//208 elements
rule ("setDifficulty") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        setDifficulty;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(difficultySetting, Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)))); // 7 elements
        "Difficulty: Insanity"
        If(Compare(Global.challengeSetting, >=, 4)); // 6 elements
            "Bot Health Values"
            Set Global Variable(botHealthList, Array Slice(Global.numbersList, 84, 19)); // 7 elements
            "Bot bite damage"
            Set Global Variable(botBiteDamageList, Array Slice(Global.numbersList, 103, 21)); // 7 elements
            "Bot bite durations"
            Set Global Variable(botBiteDurationList, Array Slice(Global.numbersList, 124, 21)); // 7 elements
            "Bot throttle speeds"
            Set Global Variable(botThrottleSpeedList, Empty Array); // 1 element
            "insanityThrottleHeroIndex: hero-entry counter, to fill all 21 Insanity throttle speeds with 1."
            For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, 21, True); // 3 elements
                Modify Global Variable(botThrottleSpeedList, Append To Array, 1); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            "Bot damage dealt"
            Set Global Variable(botDamageDealtList, Array Slice(Global.numbersList, 145, 21)); // 7 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Skip If(Compare(Total Time Elapsed, <, 100), True); // 5 elements
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String(" Difficulty adjusted for player count, change affects new zombies. ")); // 7 elements
            //lbl_0:
            "Shared HP for 1-4 players; only Winston (index 18) changes below."
            Set Global Variable(botHealthList, Array Slice(Global.numbersList, 166, 19)); // 7 elements
            "Shared bite durations for 3+ players; 1 and 2 players override them."

//92 elements
rule ("setPrematchTimer") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        setPrematchTimer;
    }
    actions {
        "Cancel if hidden timer has not been set"
        Abort If(Not(Global.menuFinaleMotionSlot)); // 3 elements
        "Voting in progress"
        If(Global.isVoting); // 2 elements
            "Event player is voting"
            If(Compare((Event Player).isPregameVoting, ==, True)); // 6 elements
                "Set match time to hidden timer"
                Set Match Time(Global.menuFinaleMotionSlot); // 2 elements
            "Event Player is not voting"
            Else; // 1 element
                "All players are finished voting"
                If(And(Compare(Match Time, >, 35), Is True For All(All Players(Team 1), And(Compare((Current Array Element).isPregameVoting, ==, False), Compare((Current Array Element).pregameState, >, Null))))); // 23 elements
                    Set Match Time(35); // 2 elements
                Else If(And(Compare(Match Time, >, 50), Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(Compare((Current Array Element).isPregameVoting, ==, False), Compare((Current Array Element).pregameState, >, Null)))), ==, Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), (Current Array Element).isPlayerActive))))); // 34 elements
                    Set Match Time(50); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        "Voting complete"
        Else; // 1 element
            "Event Player deselects hero"
            If(Compare((Event Player).pregameState, <, 2)); // 7 elements
                Set Match Time(Global.menuFinaleMotionSlot); // 2 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Call Subroutine(shortenPrematchTimer); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//54 elements
rule ("shortenPrematchTimer") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        shortenPrematchTimer;
    }
    actions {
        "Shared by END VOTING and player menu updates; no event-player context is required."
        If(And(Compare(Match Time, >, 10), Is True For All(All Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).pregameState, ==, 2)))); // 17 elements
            Set Match Time(15); // 2 elements
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Set Match Time(10); // 2 elements
        Else If(And(Compare(Match Time, >, 20), Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).pregameState, ==, 2))), ==, Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), (Current Array Element).isPlayerActive))))); // 28 elements
            Set Match Time(20); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//19 elements
rule ("enableDisableAbilities") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        enableDisableAbilities;
    }
    actions {
        If(Global.isPregameSetup); // 2 elements
            Disable Hero HUD(Event Player); // 1 element
            Set Primary Fire Enabled(Event Player, False); // 1 element
            Set Secondary Fire Enabled(Event Player, False); // 1 element
            Set Ability 1 Enabled(Event Player, False); // 1 element
            Set Ability 2 Enabled(Event Player, False); // 1 element
            Set Ultimate Ability Enabled(Event Player, False); // 1 element
            Set Melee Enabled(Event Player, False); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Primary Fire Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Set Secondary Fire Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Set Ability 1 Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Set Ability 2 Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Set Ultimate Ability Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Set Melee Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Enable Hero HUD(Event Player); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 1 element
        "Updates global objective entities"
        Else If(Compare(Global.objectiveValue, ==, 4)); // 6 elements

            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
        "Updates global objective entities"
        Else If(Compare(Global.objectiveValue, ==, 5)); // 6 elements
            "Kill Zone Progress Bar"
            Create Progress Bar HUD Text(All Players(All Teams), Multiply(Divide(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, 50), 100), Custom String("Kill Zone Elimination Progress"), Top, 2, Color(Red), Color(Red), Visible To and Values, Default Visibility); // 17 elements

            Create Icon(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.objectiveMotionSlot), >, Global.objectiveRad)), Add(Global.objectiveMotionSlot, Up), Arrow: Down, Visible To, Color(Red), True); // 16 elements

            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
        "Updates global objective entities"

            "Zombie Egg Progress Bar"

            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            "Current terminal repair percentage"
            Create In-World Text(Global.objectivePlayerList, Custom String("{0}%", Round To Integer(Value In Array(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, Index Of Array Value(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Distance Between(Local Player, Current Array Element))))), Down)), Update Every Frame(Add(Eye Position(Local Player), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Horizontal Facing Angle Of(Local Player), Add(Vertical Facing Angle Of(Local Player), 15)), 200))), 2, Do Not Clip, Visible To Position and String, Global.objectiveColor, Default Visibility); // 36 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 1 element
            "Terminal effects\nsatelliteTerminalVisualIndex: terminal index, to create its icon, repair ring, screen and distance text."
            For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of(Global.objectiveProgressSlot), True); // 4 elements
                "Terminal icon"
                Create Icon(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0))), >, Global.objectiveRad), Compare(Value In Array(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), <, 100))), Add(Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), Up), Bolt, Visible To and Position, Global.objectiveColor, True); // 38 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                "Terminal repair area ring"
                Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring, Global.objectiveColor, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), Global.objectiveRad, Visible To Position and Radius); // 11 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                "Terminal \"screen\" light shaft"
                Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Light Shaft, Global.objectiveColor, Add(Add(Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), Global.currentObjectivePosition), Global.objectiveRad), 0.8)), Multiply(-1.5, Up)), 0.25, Visible To Position and Radius); // 33 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                "Objective distance"
                Create In-World Text(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), And(Compare(Value In Array(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), <, 100), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0))), >, Global.objectiveRad))), Custom String("Distance: {0}m", Distance Between(Local Player, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)))), Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), True, Do Not Clip, Visible To Position and String, Global.objectiveColor, Visible Never); // 48 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(isObjectiveComplete, False); // 1 element
    }
}

//170 elements
rule ("selectTarget") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        selectTarget;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot1, True); // 1 element
        "botSelectionCandidateList: per-call target calculations; preserve the final snapshot below for the boss distance text."
        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Empty Array); // 1 element
        "Miniboss prioritizes players on objective"
        If(Array Contains(Global.miniBossList, Event Player)); // 4 elements
            Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Append To Array, Filtered Array(Global.objectivePlayerList, Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))); // 6 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "Prioitize dummy bots"
        Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Append To Array, Filtered Array(Global.playerTargetList, Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))); // 5 elements
        "For projectile firing heroes"
        If(And(Not(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player)), Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Pharah)))); // 12 elements
            Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Append To Array, Filtered Array(Global.playerTargetList, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Nearest Walkable Position(Position Of(Current Array Element))), >, 5))); // 12 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "Add players who are below bot to target list"
        Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Append To Array, Sorted Array(Filtered Array(Remove From Array(Global.playerTargetList, Global.tmp1), Compare(Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), <, 2.5)), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))); // 21 elements
        "Add remaining players"
        Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Append To Array, Sorted Array(Remove From Array(Global.playerTargetList, Global.tmp1), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))); // 9 elements
        "Remove invisible players"
        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Filtered Array(Global.tmp1, Not((Current Array Element).invisibilityCount))); // 7 elements
        "The reevaluated boss distance-text visibility reads this per-bot snapshot after this call returns."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, candidateTargetList, Global.tmp1); // 2 elements
        "botSelectionCandidateIndex: candidate-target index, to select the first target visible to this bot.\nOnly a candidate that passed line of sight may become the search result."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot25, Null); // 1 element
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of(Global.tmp1), True); // 4 elements
            If(Is In Line of Sight(Eye Position(Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp0)), Add(Position Of(Event Player), Global.losOffsetConst), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)); // 13 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot25, Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp0)); // 5 elements
                Break; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        If(Is Alive(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot25, !=, Null)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot5, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot25); // 3 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot21, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5); // 3 elements
                Start Rule(restoreDefaultFacingMovement, Restart Rule); // 1 element
            Else If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, !=, Null)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot5, Null); // 1 element
                Skip If((Event Player).actorTeamSlot15, True); // 3 elements
                Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Event Player, Add(Position Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), Multiply(Normalize(World Vector Of(Throttle Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), (Event Player).actorTeamSlot21, Rotation)), -2))), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, To World, Replace existing throttle, None); // 22 elements
                //__label_if_4__:
                Skip If((Event Player).actorTeamSlot14, True); // 3 elements
                Start Facing(Event Player, Throttle Of(Event Player), If-Then-Else((Event Player).isCrowdControlled, Null, 100), To Player, Direction and Turn Rate); // 8 elements
                //__label_if_5__:
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot1, False); // 1 element
    }
}

//74 elements
rule ("restoreDefaultFacingMovement") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        restoreDefaultFacingMovement;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, !=, Null)); // 6 elements
            Skip If((Event Player).actorTeamSlot14, True); // 3 elements
            Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5)), If-Then-Else((Event Player).isAttached, 1500, If-Then-Else((Event Player).isCrowdControlled, Null, 100)), To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 19 elements
            //__label_if_6__:
            Skip If((Event Player).actorTeamSlot15, True); // 3 elements
            Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5), If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Vector(X Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5)), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Z Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5)))), >, Multiply(2, Min(True, (Event Player).sizeScale))), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, Null), To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude); // 35 elements
            //__label_if_7__:
        Else; // 1 element
            Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Facing Direction Of(Event Player), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, To World, Replace existing throttle, None); // 4 elements
            Call Subroutine(faceCurrentThrottle); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//1088 elements
rule ("botRespawnInitialization") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        botRespawnInitialization;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot18, True); // 1 element
        If(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player)); // 4 elements
            Disable Nameplates(Event Player, All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, Null); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, initialMaxHealth, Max Health(Event Player)); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Enable Nameplates(Event Player, All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, Workshop Setting Real(Custom String("Zombies"), Custom String("Zombie Size"), 1, 0, 20, 0)); // 16 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, Value In Array(Global.botHealthList, Index Of Array Value(Global.botHeroList, Hero Of(Event Player)))); // 8 elements
            "Scale zombie voice lines with size"
            Start Modifying Hero Voice Lines(Event Player, Add(Add(0.5, Divide(True, Add((Event Player).sizeScale, True))), Multiply(0.25, Sine From Degrees(Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, Evaluate Once(Random Real(360, 720)))))), True); // 24 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Crouch)); // 2 elements
        Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)); // 2 elements
        Enable Movement Collision With Environment(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot13, True); // 1 element
        Enable Movement Collision With Players(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isAttached, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot14, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot15, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot9, True); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isCrowdControlled, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorAbilitySlot1, Null); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorAbilitySlot2, Null); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot10, Null); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot1, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot12, -1); // 2 elements
        "Chance for a minboss on high difficulties"
        If(And(And(Compare(Global.difficultySetting, >=, 3), Not(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player))), Not(Is True For Any(Global.miniBossList, Entity Exists(Current Array Element))))); // 19 elements
            If(Compare(Random Integer(True, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.phaseSlot10, ==, 1), 27, 189)), ==, 1)); // 17 elements
                Modify Global Variable(miniBossList, Append To Array, Event Player); // 1 element
                Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Random Value In Array(Array(Hero(Reinhardt), Hero(Wrecking Ball), Hero(Junker Queen), Hero(Doomfist), Hero(Kiriko), Hero(Mauga), Hero(Hazard), Hero(Reaper)))); // 23 elements
                Skip If(Compare(Global.zombieSetting, !=, 6), True); // 6 elements
                Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Random Value In Array(Array(Hero(Reinhardt), Hero(Wrecking Ball)))); // 8 elements
                //__label_if_8__:
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, 2500); // 2 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, Multiply, 1.55); // 2 elements
                Start Forcing Player Outlines(Event Player, All Players(All Teams), True, Color(Orange), Default); // 4 elements
                Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player, Custom String("Contestor")); // 5 elements
                "Skull Icon"
                Create Icon(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), And(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot18, ==, False), Not(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player)))), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Up), Skull, Visible To and Position, Color(Orange), True); // 20 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, visualEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                Wait(2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, Index Of Array Value(Global.botHeroList, Hero Of(Event Player))); // 5 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, botBiteDamage, Value In Array(Global.botBiteDamageList, Global.tmp0)); // 5 elements
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, botBiteDamage, Multiply, Divide(100, Value In Array(Global.botDamageDealtList, Global.tmp0))); // 8 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot6, Value In Array(Global.botBiteDurationList, Global.tmp0)); // 5 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, aiThrottleSpeedSlot, Value In Array(Global.botThrottleSpeedList, Global.tmp0)); // 5 elements
        Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.botDamageDealtList, Global.tmp0), 100), 100)); // 11 elements
        Set Damage Received(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, 1); // 2 elements
        Set Move Speed(Event Player, Add(100, Multiply(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).rescueTimeRemaining, <=, Null))), 3))); // 17 elements
        Set Jump Vertical Speed(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Set Healing Received(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Set Projectile Speed(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Set Projectile Gravity(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot19, 1); // 2 elements
        Set Knockback Received(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Reinhardt))); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot19, 0.7); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(applyBotKnockbackScale); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Mercy))); // 6 elements
            Set Projectile Speed(Event Player, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player), 75, 20)); // 9 elements
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Illari))); // 6 elements
            Set Knockback Dealt(Event Player, 25); // 2 elements
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Winston))); // 6 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, Multiply, 0.7); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1, 2); // 2 elements
            Start Scaling Barriers(Event Player, (Event Player).sizeScale, True); // 3 elements
        Else If(Array Contains(Array(Hero(Ana), Hero(Kiriko)), Hero Of(Event Player))); // 10 elements
            Set Projectile Gravity(Event Player, 500); // 2 elements
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Sigma))); // 6 elements
            Set Projectile Speed(Event Player, Multiply(Multiply(100, (Event Player).sizeScale), 0.5)); // 9 elements
            Set Primary Fire Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Mauga))); // 6 elements
            Set Knockback Dealt(Event Player, 120); // 2 elements
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Hazard))); // 6 elements
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, botAbilityCooldown, False, True, Destination and Rate); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Anran))); // 6 elements
            Set Knockback Dealt(Event Player, 150); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Skip If(Array Contains(Global.miniBossList, Event Player), 36); // 5 elements
        If(And(And(Compare(Global.availableMutationList, !=, Empty Array), Not(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player))), Compare(Random Real(0, 100), <, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Array(3, 5), Global.challengeSetting), 100, Workshop Setting Real(Custom String("Mutations"), Custom String("Chance to mutate percentage"), 25, 0, 100, 0))))); // 47 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot17, Random Value In Array(Global.availableMutationList)); // 3 elements
            "botMutationOutlineColor: outline color; every enabled mutation assigns it before the capture below.\nSpeed"
            If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot17, ==, 1)); // 7 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, Multiply, 1.5); // 2 elements
                Call Subroutine(applyScaledMoveSpeed); // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Color(Purple)); // 2 elements
                Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player, Custom String("SPEED")); // 5 elements
            "Size Growth"
            Else If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot17, ==, 2)); // 7 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, Multiply, 1.5); // 2 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, Multiply, (Event Player).sizeScale); // 3 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Color(Violet)); // 2 elements
                Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player, Custom String("XL")); // 5 elements
            "Size Reduction"
            Else If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot17, ==, 3)); // 7 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, Multiply, 0.6); // 2 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, Multiply, (Event Player).sizeScale); // 3 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Color(Rose)); // 2 elements
                Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player, Custom String("XS")); // 5 elements
            "Radioactive"
            Else If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot17, ==, 4)); // 7 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot6, Multiply, 1.5); // 2 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Color(Lime Green)); // 2 elements
                Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player, Custom String("Radioactive")); // 5 elements
            "Heavy: enabled mutation IDs are 1..5, so the remaining case is 5."
            Else; // 1 element
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot19, Multiply, 0.5); // 2 elements
                Call Subroutine(applyBotKnockbackScale); // 1 element
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, Multiply, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot19); // 3 elements
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, Multiply(100, (Event Player).movementSpeedScale)); // 6 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, Divide, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot19); // 3 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Color(Black)); // 2 elements
                Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player, Custom String("Heavy")); // 5 elements
            End; // 1 element
            "Capture the color so later scratch writes cannot change this persistent outline."
            Start Forcing Player Outlines(Event Player, All Players(All Teams), True, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp2), Default); // 6 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot17, Null); // 1 element
            "Default ouline for players"
            Stop Forcing Player Outlines(Event Player, All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
            Stop Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        //lbl_0:
        Call Subroutine(applyCurrentSizeScale); // 1 element

//137 elements
rule ("explosionSequence") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        explosionSequence;
    }
    actions {
        "Filter the chosen team for both explosion types; dummy bot explosions also require line of sight."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, abilityTargetList, Filtered Array(If-Then-Else(Is Dummy Bot(Event Player), All Players(All Teams), All Players(Team 2)), And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, Multiply((Event Player).explosionScale, 6)), If-Then-Else(Is Dummy Bot(Event Player), Is In Line of Sight(Eye Position(Current Array Element), Eye Position(Event Player), Enemy Barriers Block LOS), True)))); // 32 elements
        "Scaled"
        Damage((Event Player).abilityTargetList, Event Player, If-Then-Else(Is Dummy Bot(Event Player), Multiply((Event Player).botBiteDamage, 2.5), Multiply((Event Player).infectionLevel, 100))); // 17 elements
        If(Is Dummy Bot(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            "Scaled"
            Damage(Filtered Array((Event Player).abilityTargetList, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, Multiply(3, (Event Player).sizeScale))), Event Player, Multiply((Event Player).botBiteDamage, 5)); // 20 elements
            "Scaled"
            Set Status((Event Player).abilityTargetList, Event Player, Burning, 3); // 4 elements
            "Not scaled damage"
            Start Damage Over Time((Event Player).abilityTargetList, Event Player, 3, Multiply((Event Player).botBiteDamage, 2)); // 9 elements
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).damageOverTimeRemaining, >=, 3), True); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, damageOverTimeRemaining, 3); // 2 elements
            //__label_if_9__:
        End; // 1 element
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, (Event Player).explosionOuterColor, Position Of(Event Player), Multiply(6, (Event Player).explosionScale)); // 11 elements
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, (Event Player).explosionInnerColor, Position Of(Event Player), Multiply(3, (Event Player).explosionScale)); // 11 elements
        "Both synchronous sounds use the same radius before the wait."
        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Multiply(150, (Event Player).explosionScale)); // 6 elements
        "Scaled"
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Explosion Sound, Color(Yellow), Position Of(Event Player), Global.tmp1); // 6 elements
        "Scaled"
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion Sound, Color(Yellow), Position Of(Event Player), Global.tmp1); // 6 elements
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
    }
}

//152 elements
rule ("SPAWN BOTS") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        Server Load Average <= Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("General"), Custom String("Spawn Zombies When Server Load Average is Less Than"), 180, 100, 255, 2); // 16 elements
        "@Condition getNumberOfPlayers(Team.2) < numberOfBots"
        Number Of Players(All Teams) < 24; // 4 elements
        Or(And(Compare(Global.botSpawnTimeList, !=, Empty Array), Compare(First Of(Global.botSpawnTimeList), <=, Null)), Compare(Add(Count Of(Global.botSpawnTimeList), Number Of Players(Team 2)), <, Global.numberOfBots)) == True; // 24 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, Filtered Array(Global.botHeroList, Number Of Heroes(Current Array Element, Team 2))); // 7 elements
        If(Global.isSmolderEventActive); // 2 elements
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Hero(Winston)); // 2 elements
        Else If(Compare(Count Of(Global.tmp0), <, Subtract(Global.uniqueSkinCap, Max(Number Of Slots(Team 1), Number Of Players(Team 1))))); // 15 elements
            "botSpawnWeightRoll: random threshold for weighted hero selection; the hero list is no longer needed."
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Random Real(0, 100)); // 5 elements
            "botSpawnCumulativeWeight: cumulative spawn weight, compared with botSpawnWeightRoll to find the selected hero."
            Set Global Variable(tmp1, Null); // 1 element
            "botSpawnHeroIndex: hero-table index, starting before the first entry so the loop visits index 0."
            Set Global Variable(tmp2, -1); // 2 elements
            While(Or(Compare(Global.tmp0, >, Global.tmp1), Compare(Global.tmp2, <, Null))); // 12 elements
                Modify Global Variable(tmp2, Add, True); // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Add, Value In Array(Global.botSpawnRateList, Global.tmp2)); // 5 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Value In Array(Global.botHeroList, Global.tmp2)); // 5 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Last Of(Sorted Array(Global.botHeroList, Value In Array(Global.botSpawnRateList, Current Array Index)))); // 8 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Create Dummy Bot(Global.tmp0, Team 2, -1, Global.dummyHoldingPositionConst, Null); // 5 elements
        If(And(Compare(Global.botSpawnTimeList, !=, Empty Array), Compare(First Of(Global.botSpawnTimeList), <=, Null))); // 12 elements
            Modify Global Variable(botSpawnTimeList, Remove From Array By Index, False); // 1 element
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Wait(3, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
        Wait(Last Of(Sorted Array(Global.botSpawnTimeList, Current Array Element)), Restart When True); // 5 elements
        "Reset spawn times array if conditions are not met"
        Skip If(Compare(Global.botSpawnTimeList, ==, Empty Array), True); // 5 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(botSpawnTimeList, False, Null); // 1 element
        //__label_if_10__:
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

    }
}

        Set Match Time(3599); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(And(Is True For Any(All Players(Team 1), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)), Compare(Global.mapGridList, !=, Empty Array)), 999999999999); // 13 elements
        Set Match Time(60); // 2 elements
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(menuFinaleMotionSlot, Match Time); // 1 element
        Chase Global Variable At Rate(menuFinaleMotionSlot, False, True, None); // 1 element
        Disable Built-In Game Mode Announcer; // 1 element
        Disable Built-In Game Mode Completion; // 1 element
    }
}

//85 elements
rule ("PREGAME SPAWN ROOM ROTATION") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isPregameSetup == True; // 2 elements
        "Does not apply if game mode is special gamemode"
        Global.isSpecialGameMode != True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(False, Abort When False); // 1 element
        Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Ana), Team 2, -1, Multiply(500, Up), Null); // 7 elements
        Set Global Variable(attackSpawnRooms, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(spawnRoomIndex, Null); // 1 element
        "Records spawn room locations"
        While(Compare(Count Of(Global.attackSpawnRooms), <, If-Then-Else(Compare(Current Game Mode, ==, Game Mode(Assault)), 2, 3))); // 15 elements
            Start Forcing Spawn Room(Team 2, Global.spawnRoomIndex); // 3 elements
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Respawn(All Players(Team 2)); // 3 elements
            While(Not(Is In Spawn Room(First Of(All Players(Team 2))))); // 6 elements
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(attackSpawnRooms, Append To Array, Position Of(First Of(All Players(Team 2)))); // 5 elements
            Modify Global Variable(spawnRoomIndex, Add, True); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "Accounts for first attack spawn rooms generally being bigger than other spawn rooms"
        Modify Global Variable At Index(attackSpawnRooms, False, Add, Multiply(Direction Towards(First Of(Global.attackSpawnRooms), Objective Position(False)), 10)); // 9 elements
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Destroy All Dummy Bots; // 1 element
        "Rotates spawn rooms while waiting for game to start"
        While(Global.isPregameSetup); // 2 elements
            Set Global Variable(spawnRoomIndex, Modulo(Add(Global.spawnRoomIndex, True), Count Of(Global.attackSpawnRooms))); // 8 elements
            "Open all spawn rooms"
            Start Forcing Spawn Room(All Teams, Global.spawnRoomIndex); // 3 elements
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "Change active spawn room to a location"
        Set Global Variable(activeAttackSpawnRoom, Value In Array(Global.attackSpawnRooms, Global.spawnRoomIndex)); // 5 elements
    }
}

//52 elements
rule ("START GAME") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isPregameSetup != False; // 2 elements
        Match Time <= 0.1; // 2 elements
        Is True For Any(All Players(Team 1), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)) == True; // 6 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Is In Setup); // 1 element
            Set Match Time(False); // 1 element
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Disable Built-In Game Mode Scoring; // 1 element
        Disable Built-In Game Mode Announcer; // 1 element
        Pause Match Time; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(walkCenter, Nearest Walkable Position(Global.walkCenter)); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable(isPregameSetup, False); // 1 element
        Enable Built-In Game Mode Music; // 1 element
        Disable Inspector Recording; // 1 element
        "enableInspector()"
        Destroy All Dummy Bots; // 1 element
        Destroy All Effects; // 1 element
        Destroy All HUD Text; // 1 element
        Destroy All Icons; // 1 element
        Destroy All In-World Text; // 1 element
        "Keep the pregame camera until PLAYER INITIALIZATION finishes placement and facing.\nIts coordinates have independent storage, so publishing objective caches cannot move it."
        Call Subroutine(refreshCurrentObjectiveType); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(refreshCurrentObjectivePosition); // 1 element
        "Enhancement icons no longer have live menu readers; restore the goal text's initial value."
        Set Global Variable(phaseSlot9, Null); // 1 element
        "Preserve the color's original value before the first biogoal."
        Set Global Variable(biomorphingGoalColor, Null); // 1 element
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Set Match Time(Add(300, Global.matchEndTime)); // 5 elements
        Unpause Match Time; // 1 element
        "Disabled survivor spawn room healing zone"
        Start Forcing Spawn Room(Team 1, Color(Blue)); // 3 elements
        "Objective Description"
        Set Objective Description(All Players(All Teams), Global.objectiveDescription, Visible To and String); // 4 elements
        Wait(5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Disable Built-In Game Mode Music; // 1 element
    }
}

//140 elements
rule ("generateMapGrid") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        generateMapGrid;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(mapGridList, Empty Array); // 1 element
        While(Compare(Count Of(Global.mapGridList), <, 900)); // 7 elements
            "mapGridCandidatePosition: candidate walkable grid point, to check spawn-room clearance and point spacing."
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Nearest Walkable Position(Add(If-Then-Else(Compare(Count Of(Global.mapGridList), >, 5), Random Value In Array(Global.mapGridList), Global.walkCenter), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Random Real(0, 360), Random Real(-35, 35)), Random Real(10, 100))))); // 32 elements
            "Keep grid points outside both spawn rooms on these five game modes."
            If(Or(Not(Array Contains(Array(Game Mode(Capture The Flag), Game Mode(Clash), Game Mode(Push), Game Mode(Control), Game Mode(Flashpoint)), Current Game Mode)), And(Is True For All(Spawn Points(Team 2), Compare(Distance Between(Position Of(Current Array Element), Global.tmp0), >, 50)), Is True For All(Spawn Points(Team 1), Compare(Distance Between(Position Of(Current Array Element), Global.tmp0), >, 40))))); // 43 elements
                If(Or(Compare(Global.mapGridList, ==, Empty Array), Is True For All(Global.mapGridList, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.tmp0), >, 2.5)))); // 17 elements
                    Modify Global Variable(mapGridList, Append To Array, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            If(And(Global.isPregameSetup, Compare(Add(Match Time, Global.matchEndTime), <, 2))); // 11 elements
                Break; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "0: Consecutive, 1: Minimum"
        Set Global Variable(objectiveSpacingList, Array(Min(60, Divide(Count Of(Global.mapGridList), 11)), Min(30, Divide(Count Of(Global.mapGridList), 20)))); // 20 elements
    }
}

//327 elements
rule ("GLOBAL MENU INITIALIZATION") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isPregameSetup == True; // 2 elements
        Global.mapGridList != Empty Array; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(False, Abort When False); // 1 element
        If(Global.isSpecialGameMode); // 2 elements
            "These values are consumed before the next wait; mode selection keeps the original three positions."
            If(Array Contains(Array(Game Mode(Capture The Flag), Game Mode(Push)), Current Game Mode)); // 8 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp1, 300); // 2 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, 15); // 2 elements
            Else If(Compare(Current Game Mode, ==, Game Mode(Control))); // 5 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp1, 300); // 2 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, 100); // 2 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(tmp1, 150); // 2 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, 30); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(startPos, Add(Add(Global.walkCenter, Multiply(Direction From Angles(Random Real(0, 360), 0), Global.tmp1)), Multiply(Global.tmp2, Up))); // 19 elements
            Set Global Variable(startCamPos, Add(Add(Global.startPos, Global.offsetUpOneAndHalfConst), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Global.walkCenter, Global.startPos)), 0), 6.5))); // 18 elements
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Abort If(Not(Global.isPregameSetup)); // 3 elements
            Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Reaper), Team 2, -1, Nearest Walkable Position(Global.startPos), Forward); // 6 elements
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Abort If(Not(Global.isPregameSetup)); // 3 elements
            Set Invisible(All Players(Team 2), All); // 3 elements
            Start Forcing Player Position(First Of(All Players(Team 2)), Add(Global.startPos, Multiply(15, Up)), True); // 10 elements
            Start Facing(All Players(Team 2), Forward, 100000, To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 4 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            While(Compare(Global.startPos, ==, Null)); // 5 elements
                Set Global Variable(startPos, Objective Position(Random Integer(False, If-Then-Else(Compare(Current Game Mode, !=, Game Mode(Assault)), 2, 1)))); // 13 elements
                If(Compare(Current Game Mode, ==, Game Mode(Clash))); // 5 elements
                    Set Global Variable(startPos, Spawn Points(Team 1)); // 3 elements
                    If(Compare(Current Map, ==, Map(Throne of Anubis))); // 5 elements
                        Set Global Variable(startPos, Vector(-0.27, 0.49, 83.6)); // 7 elements
                        Set Global Variable(startCamPos, Vector(-0.19, 2, 90.1)); // 7 elements
                        Skip(18); // 2 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(startCamPos, Add(Add(Global.startPos, Global.offsetUpOneAndHalfConst), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Random Real(0, 360), 0), 6.5))); // 17 elements
                Set Global Variable(startCamPos, Add(Ray Cast Hit Position(Global.startCamPos, Subtract(Global.startCamPos, Multiply(5, Up)), Null, Null, False), Global.offsetUpOneAndHalfConst)); // 16 elements
                Set Global Variable(startPos, Vector(X Component Of(Global.startPos), Subtract(Y Component Of(Global.startCamPos), 1.5), Z Component Of(Global.startPos))); // 13 elements
                Set Global Variable(startPos, Ray Cast Hit Position(Add(Global.startPos, Multiply(0.4, Up)), Subtract(Global.startPos, Multiply(5, Up)), Null, Null, False)); // 18 elements
                Set Global Variable(startPos, Subtract(Ray Cast Hit Position(Subtract(Global.startCamPos, Global.offsetUpThreeQuartersConst), Add(Global.startPos, Global.offsetUpThreeQuartersConst), Null, Null, False), Global.offsetUpThreeQuartersConst)); // 17 elements
                Set Global Variable(startCamPos, Add(Add(Global.startPos, Global.offsetUpOneAndHalfConst), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Global.startPos, Global.startCamPos)), 0), 6.5))); // 18 elements
                "Line of Sight Checks for each player position\nmenuVisibilityAngleOffset: horizontal angle offset in degrees, to test visibility of each menu position."
                For Global Variable(tmp0, -26, 27, 2); // 4 elements
                    "Both sight checks use the same point in this iteration, before any wait."
                    Set Global Variable(tmp1, Add(Add(Global.startCamPos, Multiply(-0.75, Up)), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Add(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Global.startCamPos, Global.startPos)), Global.tmp0), 0), 6.5))); // 23 elements
                    Skip If(And(Is In Line of Sight(Global.startCamPos, Global.tmp1, Barriers Do Not Block LOS), Is In Line of Sight(Global.tmp1, Global.startCamPos, Barriers Do Not Block LOS)), 2); // 14 elements
                    Set Global Variable(startPos, Null); // 1 element
                    Break; // 1 element
                    //__label_if_14__:
                End; // 1 element
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                Abort If(Not(Global.isPregameSetup)); // 3 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        //lbl_0:
        "Scene setup can resume after START GAME; the angle slot then belongs to the objective type cache."
        If(Global.isPregameSetup); // 2 elements
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot10, 40); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "Start screen objective description"
        Set Objective Description(All Players(All Teams), If-Then-Else(Global.isVoting, Custom String("Voting ends in {0}s", Round To Integer(Subtract(Match Time, 30), Up)), Custom String("Select your hero")), Visible To and String); // 20 elements
    }
}

//54 elements
rule ("END VOTING") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isVoting == True; // 2 elements
        Global.isPregameSetup == True; // 2 elements
        Match Time < 30; // 2 elements

    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(menuFinaleMotionSlot, 30); // 2 elements
        Start Rule(determineZombieMode, Restart Rule); // 1 element
        "Waits until votes have been counted"
        Wait Until(Not(And(Global.isVoting, Global.isPregameSetup)), 999999999999); // 7 elements
        "Votes share runtime trade/path storage; a resumed menu writer must not touch it."
        Abort If(Not(Global.isPregameSetup)); // 3 elements
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), actorTeamSlot3, Global.challengeSetting); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), menuTradeItemSlot, Global.zombieSetting); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), actorTeamSlot21, Global.objectiveTypeSetting); // 4 elements
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Voting has ended!")); // 7 elements
        "Reset the timer, then apply readiness even if nobody changes state after voting."
        If(Global.menuFinaleMotionSlot); // 2 elements
            Set Match Time(Global.menuFinaleMotionSlot); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(shortenPrematchTimer); // 1 element
    }
}

//47 elements
rule ("determineZombieMode") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        determineZombieMode;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(zombieSetting, Null); // 1 element
        "zombieModeVoteIndex: zombie-mode vote index, to count active players voting for each enabled mode."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 1, Count Of(Global.phaseSlot5), True); // 4 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(phaseSlot5, Global.tmp0, If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot5, Global.tmp0), True), Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And((Current Array Element).isPlayerActive, Compare((Current Array Element).menuTradeItemSlot, ==, Global.tmp0)))), -1)); // 27 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Global.phaseSlot5); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(tmp2, Global.zombieSetting); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(selectVoteWinner); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(zombieSetting, Global.tmp2); // 2 elements
        "If all players choose no preference, randomize"
        Skip If(Global.zombieSetting, True); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(zombieSetting, 1); // 2 elements
        //__label_if_15__:
        Start Rule(determineChallenge, Restart Rule); // 1 element
        Start Rule(setZombieMode, Restart Rule); // 1 element
    }
}

            Set Global Variable(botSpawnRateList, Array Slice(Global.numbersList, Value In Array(Array(Null, 19, Null, 38, 57, 74), Subtract(Global.zombieSetting, True)), Value In Array(Array(19, 19, Null, 19, 17, 10), Subtract(Global.zombieSetting, True)))); // 38 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//121 elements
rule ("determineChallenge") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        determineChallenge;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(challengeSetting, Null); // 1 element
        "challengeVoteIndex: challenge vote index, to count active players voting for each enabled challenge."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 1, Count Of(Global.phaseSlot3), True); // 4 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(phaseSlot3, Global.tmp0, If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.tmp0), True), Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And((Current Array Element).isPlayerActive, Compare((Current Array Element).actorTeamSlot3, ==, Global.tmp0)))), -1)); // 27 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "challengeWeightedVoteSum: sum of challenge IDs weighted by votes, to calculate the average challenge vote."
        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Null); // 1 element
        "challengeVoteIndex: challenge index, to include each challenge's weighted votes in challengeWeightedVoteSum."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 1, Count Of(Global.phaseSlot3), True); // 4 elements
            Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Add, Multiply(Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.tmp0), Global.tmp0)); // 8 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "challengeAverageVote: average challenge vote, to choose the closer challenge when vote counts tie."
        Modify Global Variable(tmp1, Divide, Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).actorTeamSlot3, >, Null)))); // 11 elements
        "challengeVoteIndex: candidate challenge index, to choose the winner using votes and distance from challengeAverageVote."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 1, Count Of(Global.phaseSlot3), True); // 4 elements
            "If challenge being compared has more votes than current challenge"
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.challengeSetting), <, Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.tmp0))); // 12 elements
                Set Global Variable(challengeSetting, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
            Else If(And(Compare(Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.tmp0), >, Null), Compare(Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.challengeSetting), ==, Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot3, Global.tmp0)))); // 21 elements
                Skip If(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Global.challengeSetting, Global.tmp1)), <=, Absolute Value(Subtract(Global.tmp0, Global.tmp1))), True); // 14 elements
                Set Global Variable(challengeSetting, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
                //__label_if_16__:
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "Default challenge"
        Skip If(Global.challengeSetting, True); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(challengeSetting, 1); // 2 elements
        //__label_if_17__:
        Start Rule(determineObjectiveType, Restart Rule); // 1 element
    }
}

//43 elements
rule ("Subroutine selectVoteWinner") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        selectVoteWinner;
    }
    actions {
        "Keep the original traversal and sequential coin flips; a uniform pick among tied options differs.\nCallers publish the result immediately, then handle their own no-preference defaults."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 1, Count Of(Global.tmp1), True); // 4 elements
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp2), <, Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp0))); // 12 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
            Else If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp2), ==, Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp0))); // 12 elements
                If(And(Global.tmp2, Not(Random Integer(False, True)))); // 7 elements
                    Set Global Variable(tmp2, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//195 elements
rule ("setObjectiveType") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        setObjectiveType;
    }
    actions {
        "Wait until game begins"
        Wait Until(And(Is Game In Progress, Not(Global.isPregameSetup)), 999999999999); // 6 elements
        "Create objective positions"
        While(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectiveTypeList), <, Add(Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("General"), Custom String("Number of Objectives"), 5, 0, 100, 0), True))); // 23 elements
            If(Compare(Global.objectiveTypeSetting, !=, 9)); // 6 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveTypeList, Append To Array, Global.objectiveTypeSetting); // 2 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveTypeList, Append To Array, Random Value In Array(Remove From Array(Global.phaseSlot8, Filtered Array(Global.objectiveTypeList, Compare(Current Array Index, >, Subtract(Subtract(Count Of(Global.objectiveTypeList), True), Subtract(Count Of(Global.phaseSlot8), True))))))); // 21 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(refreshCurrentObjectiveType); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot1, Null); // 1 element
            While(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), <, Count Of(Global.objectiveTypeList))); // 8 elements
                "Limits possible map grid points to set min and max range from last created objective position"
                Set Global Variable(objectivePlacementSlot, Random Value In Array(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.objectivePositionList, ==, Empty Array), Global.mapGridList, Filtered Array(Global.mapGridList, And(Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Last Of(Global.objectivePositionList)), >, First Of(Global.objectiveSpacingList)), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Last Of(Global.objectivePositionList)), <, Min(100, Multiply(First Of(Global.objectiveSpacingList), 3)))))))); // 39 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectivePlacementSlot, Add, Up); // 1 element
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                "Minimum objective spacing"
                If(Or(Compare(Global.objectivePositionList, ==, Empty Array), Is True For All(Array Slice(Global.objectivePositionList, Subtract(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), 5), 5), Compare(Distance Between(Global.objectivePlacementSlot, Current Array Element), >, Value In Array(Global.objectiveSpacingList, True))))); // 28 elements
                    "Ensures bosses spawn with head room"
                    If(Or(Compare(Last Of(Global.objectiveTypeList), !=, 2), Is In Line of Sight(Global.objectivePlacementSlot, Add(Global.objectivePlacementSlot, Multiply(10, Up)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))); // 19 elements
                        Modify Global Variable(objectivePositionList, Append To Array, Global.objectivePlacementSlot); // 2 elements
                        Call Subroutine(refreshCurrentObjectivePosition); // 1 element
                        Set Global Variable(phaseSlot1, Null); // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(phaseSlot1, Add, True); // 1 element
                "If Too many attempts"
                If(Compare(Global.phaseSlot1, >, 50)); // 6 elements
                    "Preserve both removals and refresh the head after each one."
                    For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, 2, True); // 3 elements
                        Modify Global Variable(objectivePositionList, Remove From Array By Index, Subtract(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), True)); // 5 elements
                        Call Subroutine(refreshCurrentObjectivePosition); // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                    Set Global Variable(phaseSlot1, Null); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "Last objective must be extraction objective"
        Set Global Variable At Index(objectiveTypeList, Subtract(Count Of(Global.objectiveTypeList), True), 1); // 6 elements
        Call Subroutine(refreshCurrentObjectiveType); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(objectivesManagement); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(isGameplayActive, True); // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("PLAYER SELECTS/RESELECTS HERO") {
    event {

    actions {
        Wait(False, Abort When False); // 1 element

            Call Subroutine(attachPlayerToPregameAnchor); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Teleport(Event Player, (Event Player).team1PositionSlot); // 3 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element

        Abort If(Not(Global.isPregameSetup)); // 3 elements
        "Set invisiblity status to 0 each time hero is selected/reselected"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, invisibilityCount, Null); // 1 element

        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isPlayerActive, True); // 1 element
        Wait(5, Restart When True); // 2 elements
        Abort If(Not(Global.isPregameSetup)); // 3 elements
        If(And(Compare((Event Player).augmentId, ==, Null), Or(And(Global.isVoting, And(Not(Or(Or((Event Player).actorTeamSlot3, (Event Player).menuTradeItemSlot), (Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), Compare((Event Player).enhancementId, ==, Null))), And(Not(Global.isVoting), Compare((Event Player).enhancementId, ==, Null))))); // 40 elements
            Press Button(Event Player, Button(Jump)); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        //__label_if_19__:
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//69 elements
rule ("PLAYER MENU CONTROLS (JUMP AND CROUCH, CHANGE HERO/READY)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isPregameSetup == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).pregameState > Null; // 3 elements
        Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Jump)), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Crouch))) == True; // 9 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        "Player indicates they are ready"
        If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Jump))); // 4 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).pregameState, <, 2)); // 7 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, pregameState, Add, True); // 1 element
                If(Compare((Event Player).pregameState, ==, 2)); // 7 elements
                    Start Rule(setPrematchTimer, Restart Rule); // 1 element
                    Start Rule(randomActions, Restart Rule); // 1 element
                    Set Invisible(Event Player, None); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        "Not ready or change hero"
        Else If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Crouch))); // 4 elements
            Set Invisible(Event Player, All); // 1 element
            If(Compare((Event Player).pregameState, >, Null)); // 6 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, pregameState, Add, -1); // 2 elements
                Start Rule(setPrematchTimer, Restart Rule); // 1 element
                If(Compare((Event Player).pregameState, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
                    "Update player voting status"
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, isPregameVoting, False); // 1 element
                    Call Subroutine(excludeCurrentHeroFromSelection); // 1 element

//58 elements
rule ("randomActions") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        randomActions;
    }
    actions {
        "pregameEmoteDirection: random emote direction 1-4, to choose one communication before the next wait."
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, Random Integer(True, 4)); // 4 elements
        "Random emote"
        If(Compare(Global.tmp0, <=, 1)); // 6 elements
            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Global.tmp0, <=, 2)); // 6 elements
            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Down); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Global.tmp0, <=, 3)); // 6 elements
            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Left); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Right); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        "pregameVoiceLineDirection: random voice-line direction 1-4, to choose one communication before the next wait."
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, Random Integer(True, 4)); // 4 elements
        "Random voice line"
        If(Compare(Global.tmp0, <=, 1)); // 6 elements
            Communicate(Event Player, Voice Line Up); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Global.tmp0, <=, 2)); // 6 elements
            Communicate(Event Player, Voice Line Down); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare(Global.tmp0, <=, 3)); // 6 elements
            Communicate(Event Player, Voice Line Left); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Communicate(Event Player, Voice Line Right); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("EFFECTS/TEXTS") {
    event {

    }
    conditions {

    actions {
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element

        Create HUD Text(All Players(All Teams), Null, Null, Custom String("{0}{1}", Global.objectiveDescription, If-Then-Else(Compare(Match Time, >, Global.matchEndTime), Custom String(" | {0}", If-Then-Else(Compare(Match Time, <=, 60), Custom String("{0}s", Match Time), Custom String("{0}:{1}{2}", Round To Integer(Divide(Match Time, 60), Down), If-Then-Else(Compare(Round To Integer(Modulo(Match Time, 60), Down), <, 10), Null, Empty Array), Round To Integer(Modulo(Match Time, 60), Down)))), Empty Array)), Top, -1, Null, Null, Custom Color(215, 215, 215, 255), Visible To and String, Default Visibility); // 65 elements
        "Infected Hud"
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), And(And(And(Compare((Current Array Element).enhancementId, !=, 2), Or(Compare(Global.healthpackPositionList, ==, Empty Array), Compare(Health(Current Array Element), <, Multiply(Raise To Power(Round To Integer((Current Array Element).infectionLevel, Down), 2), 20)))), (Current Array Element).isInfected), Compare(Match Time, >, Global.matchEndTime))), Custom String("{0} Find a healthpack {0}", Icon String(Plus)), Null, Null, Right, False, If-Then-Else(Modulo(Round To Integer(Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, 2), Up), 2), Color(White), If-Then-Else(Compare(Health(Local Player), <, Multiply(Raise To Power(Round To Integer((Local Player).infectionLevel, Down), 2), 10)), Color(Red), Color(Lime Green))), Null, Null, Visible To and Color, Default Visibility); // 79 elements
        "Nearest Healthpack Distance"
        Create In-World Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(And(And(Compare((Current Array Element).enhancementId, !=, 2), Compare(Global.healthpackPositionList, !=, Empty Array)), (Current Array Element).isInfected), Compare(Match Time, >, Global.matchEndTime))), Custom String("Cure: {0}m", Distance Between(Local Player, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.healthpackPositionList, Angle Between Vectors(Facing Direction Of(Local Player), Direction Towards(Eye Position(Local Player), Current Array Element)))))), First Of(Sorted Array(Global.healthpackPositionList, Angle Between Vectors(Facing Direction Of(Local Player), Direction Towards(Eye Position(Local Player), Current Array Element)))), True, Do Not Clip, Visible To Position and String, Color(Green), Visible Never); // 54 elements
        "Nearest health pack icon"
        Create Icon(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(And(And(Compare((Current Array Element).enhancementId, !=, 2), Compare(Global.healthpackPositionList, !=, Empty Array)), (Current Array Element).isInfected), Compare(Match Time, >, Global.matchEndTime))), Add(Add(First Of(Sorted Array(Global.healthpackPositionList, Angle Between Vectors(Facing Direction Of(Local Player), Direction Towards(Eye Position(Local Player), Current Array Element)))), Global.offsetUpThreeQuartersConst), Global.offsetUpHalfConst), Poison, Visible To and Position, Color(Green), True); // 44 elements
        Create HUD Text(All Players(All Teams), Null, Null, Custom String("Modified version. workshop.codes/786tw"), Right, 0.1, Null, Null, Color(Team 1), Visible To and String, Default Visibility); // 9 elements
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Compare(Global.challengeSetting, >=, 2)), Null, Null, Custom String("Challenge round!"), Right, 0.2, Null, Null, Color(Team 1), Visible To and String, Default Visibility); // 16 elements
    }
}

//52 elements
rule ("PLAYERS IN EXTRACTION POINT CONTROL") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }

        Global.isObjectiveComplete == False; // 2 elements

    }

        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
        If(Global.isGameplayActive); // 2 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectivePlayerList, Remove From Array By Value, Event Player); // 1 element
    }
}

//54 elements
rule ("EXTRACTION ZONE INFECTION INCREASE") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;

            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isInfected, True); // 1 element

        "Kick players out of the spawn room if dome is closing in."
        While(Is In Spawn Room(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            Teleport(Event Player, Nearest Walkable Position(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Multiply(Direction Towards(Event Player, Global.currentObjectivePosition), 10)))); // 11 elements
            Wait(0.125, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop; // 1 element
    }
}

//281 elements
rule ("WIN/LOSS CONDITIONS") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isObjectiveComplete == False; // 2 elements
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        Or(And(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), ==, 1), Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePlayerList), ==, Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(And(Has Spawned(Current Array Element), Compare((Current Array Element).rescueTimeRemaining, >, Null)), Not((Current Array Element).isAfk)))))), Or(Compare(Global.playerTargetList, ==, Empty Array), Compare(Global.extractionZoneRad, <=, Null))) == True; // 44 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(And(Compare(Global.playerTargetList, ==, Empty Array), Is True For Any(Players On Hero(Hero(Anran), Team 1), Compare(Ultimate Charge Percent(Current Array Element), ==, 100)))); // 18 elements
            Wait(5.5, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(isGameplayActive, False); // 1 element
        Enable Built-In Game Mode Announcer; // 1 element
        Disable Nameplates(All Players(All Teams), All Players(All Teams)); // 5 elements
        Disable Game Mode HUD(All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
        Disable Kill Feed(All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
        Disable Hero HUD(All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
        Start Forcing Player Outlines(All Players(All Teams), All Players(All Teams), False, Null, Default); // 5 elements
        Destroy All Effects; // 1 element
        Destroy All Icons; // 1 element
        Destroy All In-World Text; // 1 element
        Destroy All Progress Bar HUD Text; // 1 element
        Destroy All Progress Bar In-World Text; // 1 element
        Destroy All HUD Text; // 1 element
        Big Message(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(Compare(Global.playerTargetList, >, Null), Not(Array Contains(Global.objectivePlayerList, Current Array Element)))), Custom String("You were left behind!")); // 19 elements
        Set Status(All Players(Team 1), Null, Rooted, 999999999999); // 4 elements
        Set Status(All Players(Team 1), Null, Phased Out, 999999999999); // 4 elements
        If(Or(Compare(Global.playerTargetList, ==, Empty Array), Compare(Global.objectivePlayerList, ==, Empty Array))); // 11 elements
            Teleport(All Players(Team 1), Multiply(500, Up)); // 6 elements
            Set Invisible(All Players(All Teams), All); // 3 elements
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Resurrect(All Players(Team 1)); // 3 elements
            Start Camera(All Players(Team 1), Add(Global.mapGridList, Multiply(Direction From Angles(Add(Multiply(Horizontal Facing Angle Of(Local Player), -2), Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, -7.5)), -30), 200)), Global.mapGridList, 10); // 23 elements
            Start Rule(nukeMap, Do Nothing); // 1 element
            Wait(8, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Declare Team Victory(Team 2); // 2 elements
            Set Team Score(Team 2, 9); // 3 elements
            "Original author: Flashpoint needs multiple round-victory declarations to force the victory screen.\nKeep all five calls; the loop replaces repeated actions to reduce the Workshop element count."
            For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, 5, True); // 3 elements
                Declare Round Victory(Team 2); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Resurrect(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), And(Has Spawned(Current Array Element), Compare(Distance Between(Global.currentObjectivePosition, Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of(Global.currentObjectivePosition), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)))), <, Global.objectiveRad)))); // 24 elements
            Enable Kill Feed(All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot6, Add(Add(Global.currentObjectivePosition, Multiply(250, Up)), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Random Real(0, 360), 0), 500))); // 19 elements
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot7, 2); // 2 elements
            Set Global Variable(evacSurvivorList, Global.objectivePlayerList); // 2 elements
            Clear Status(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Value In Array(Global.evacSurvivorList, Current Array Element))), Rooted); // 9 elements
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot7, 10); // 2 elements
            Wait(5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot7, 2); // 2 elements
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Start Rule(nukeMap, Do Nothing); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(phaseSlot7, 50); // 2 elements
            Wait(8, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Declare Team Victory(Team 1); // 2 elements
            Set Team Score(Team 1, 9); // 3 elements
            "Original author: Flashpoint needs multiple round-victory declarations to force the victory screen.\nKeep all five calls; the loop replaces repeated actions to reduce the Workshop element count."
            For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, 5, True); // 3 elements
                Declare Round Victory(Team 1); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Enable Hero HUD(All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
        Enable Game Mode HUD(All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
    }
}

            //lbl_0:
        End; // 1 element

    }
}

        "Keeps amount of different bots within the max limit."

    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements

        Wait Until((First Of(Global.bossStateList)).actorTeamSlot18, 3); // 6 elements

        Teleport(Global.bossStateList, Global.currentObjectivePosition); // 3 elements
        While(Is True For Any(Global.bossStateList, Is Dead(Current Array Element))); // 5 elements
            Resurrect(Global.bossStateList); // 2 elements
            Wait Until(Is Alive(First Of(Global.bossStateList)), 0.25); // 5 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If(Not(Entity Exists(First Of(Global.bossStateList)))); // 5 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//174 elements
rule ("BOT BECOMES BOSS") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.phaseSlot10 == 2; // 3 elements
        Global.isObjectiveComplete == False; // 2 elements
        Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player) == True; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Modify Global Variable(miniBossList, Remove From Array By Value, Event Player); // 1 element
        If((Event Player).isAttached); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isAttached, False); // 1 element
            Detach Players(Event Player); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Invisible(Event Player, None); // 1 element
        If(Compare(Global.bossesAvailableHeroList, ==, Empty Array)); // 5 elements
            If(Compare(Global.zombieSetting, ==, 4)); // 6 elements
                Set Global Variable(bossesAvailableHeroList, Global.botHeroList); // 2 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(bossesAvailableHeroList, Filtered Array(Global.botHeroList, Compare(Value In Array(Global.botSpawnRateList, Current Array Index), >, Null))); // 10 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Skip If(Compare(Count Of(Global.bossesAvailableHeroList), <=, Count Of(Global.bossesExcludedHeroList)), True); // 8 elements
            Modify Global Variable(bossesAvailableHeroList, Remove From Array By Value, Global.bossesExcludedHeroList); // 2 elements
            //__label_if_24__:
        End; // 1 element
        Disable Nameplates(Event Player, All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
        Stop Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Event Player); // 1 element
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Random Value In Array(Global.bossesAvailableHeroList)); // 3 elements
        "Force a specific boss (FOR TESTING)\neventPlayer.startForcingHero(Hero.MERCY)"
        Call Subroutine(botRespawnInitialization); // 1 element
        "Remove Current Boss Hero From Available Boss List"
        Modify Global Variable(bossesAvailableHeroList, Remove From Array By Value, Hero Of(Event Player)); // 2 elements
        "Boss throttle speed"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, aiThrottleSpeedSlot, If-Then-Else(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Reinhardt)), 0.9, 0.65)); // 11 elements
        "Health pool size as boss"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, botBaseHealth, 15000); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot12, (Event Player).botBaseHealth); // 3 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, Workshop Setting Real(Custom String("Zombies"), Custom String("Boss Size"), 2.5, 0, 20, 0)); // 16 elements
        Start Scaling Player(Event Player, If-Then-Else(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Emre)), Add((Event Player).sizeScale, True), (Event Player).sizeScale), False); // 15 elements
        "Scale zombie voice lines with size"
        Start Modifying Hero Voice Lines(Event Player, Add(0.5, Divide(True, Add((Event Player).sizeScale, True))), False); // 10 elements
        Stop All Damage Over Time(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Health(Event Player, Max Health(Event Player)); // 2 elements
        Set Status(Event Player, Null, Rooted, 999999999999); // 2 elements
        Disable Movement Collision With Environment(Event Player, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot13, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, candidateTargetList, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)); // 2 elements
        Set Knockback Received(Event Player, 50); // 2 elements
        Set Healing Received(Event Player, 0); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Is On Ground(Event Player), 1); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(bossStateList, True, Distance Between(Event Player, Eye Position(Event Player))); // 4 elements
        Wait Until(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, ==, Null), 1); // 7 elements
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Or(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, !=, Null), Compare(Health(Event Player), <, (Event Player).botBaseHealth)), 999999999999); // 15 elements
        Clear Status(Event Player, Rooted); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(bossStateList, True, Distance Between(Event Player, Eye Position(Event Player))); // 4 elements
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Enable Movement Collision With Environment(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot13, True); // 1 element
    }
}
