//178 elements
rule ("BOSS DIES (ALL BOSS OBJECTIVES)") {
    event {
        Player Died;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dead(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player) == True; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Event Was Environment); // 1 element
            Stop All Damage Over Time(Event Player); // 1 element
            If(Compare(Team Of(Attacker), ==, Opposite Team Of(Team Of(Event Player)))); // 7 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot12, Subtract(Event Damage, 200)); // 4 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot12, Event Damage); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot12, >, Null)); // 6 elements
                While(Is Dead(Event Player)); // 2 elements
                    If(Compare(Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Y Component Of(Nearest Walkable Position(Position Of(Event Player)))), <, -50)); // 12 elements
                        "Teleport boss if they fall very far below the map"
                        Call Subroutine(bossReturn); // 1 element
                    Else; // 1 element
                        "Synchronous scratch: consume the living target before the next wait or subroutine call."
                        Set Global Variable(tmp0, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)), Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player)))); // 11 elements
                        If(Entity Exists(Global.tmp0)); // 3 elements
                            "Use the same valid target for direction and height; 17.532 is gravity acceleration."
                            Apply Impulse(Event Player, Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Event Player, Global.tmp0)), -75), Max(True, Square Root(Multiply(35.064, Subtract(Add(Y Component Of(Position Of(Global.tmp0)), 3), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)))))), To World, Cancel Contrary Motion); // 24 elements
                        End; // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                    Resurrect(Event Player); // 1 element
                    Wait Until(Is Alive(Event Player), 0.25); // 3 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Call Subroutine(applyBotBossHealth); // 1 element
                If(And(And(And(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, ==, Null), Entity Exists((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), Is Alive((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), Has Spawned((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21))); // 21 elements
                    Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, To World, Replace existing throttle, None); // 7 elements
                End; // 1 element
                "Illari punishes with ultimate"
                If(And(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Illari)), Compare(Team Of(Attacker), ==, Team 1))); // 13 elements
                    Call Subroutine(illariUlt); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            Else; // 1 element
                Call Subroutine(completeBossObjective); // 1 element
                Skip(9); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        "Resurrects player if event damage was greater than 1000 and their health still is higher than 0hp."
        Else If(Compare(Subtract(Event Damage, 1000), >, Null)); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot12, Subtract(Event Damage, 1000)); // 4 elements
            Resurrect(Event Player); // 1 element
            Wait Until(Is Alive(Event Player), 5); // 3 elements
            Call Subroutine(applyBotBossHealth); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(completeBossObjective); // 1 element
            Set Status(If-Then-Else(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Winston)), Players On Hero(Hero(Winston), Team 2), Empty Array), Null, Knocked Down, 12); // 15 elements
            //lbl_0:
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(0.25, Restart When True); // 2 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//100 elements
rule ("bossReturn") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        bossReturn;
    }
    actions {
        Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, botReturnTime); // 1 element
        Enable Movement Collision With Environment(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot2, Eye Position(Event Player)); // 2 elements
        Teleport(Event Player, Nearest Walkable Position(Event Player)); // 2 elements
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        "Select after the wait so a dead, despawned or departed previous target is replaced."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, controlStateSlot, If-Then-Else(And(And(And(Entity Exists((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), Is Alive((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), Has Spawned((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), Compare(Team Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), ==, Team 1)), (Event Player).actorTeamSlot21, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)), Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player))))); // 38 elements
        If(Entity Exists((Event Player).controlStateSlot)); // 4 elements
            "Synchronous scratch: candidate positions are consumed before the next wait."
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Filtered Array(Global.mapGridList, And(And(Is In Line of Sight(Current Array Element, Eye Position((Event Player).controlStateSlot), Barriers Do Not Block LOS), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, (Event Player).controlStateSlot), >, 5)), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, (Event Player).controlStateSlot), <, 20)))); // 30 elements
            Skip If(Compare(Global.tmp0, ==, Empty Array), True); // 5 elements
            Teleport(Event Player, Add(Up, Random Value In Array(Global.tmp0))); // 5 elements
            //__label_if_25__:
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Black), Event Player, 3); // 5 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot2, Null); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, controlStateSlot, Null); // 1 element
    }
}

            Wait(0.15, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "The resource-type slot belongs to the Rescue NPC after an objective transition."

//126 elements
rule ("PLAYER DELIVERS RESOURCE VALUE") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.phaseSlot10 == 3; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).invisibilityCount != True; // 3 elements

        Distance Between(Global.currentObjectivePosition, Event Player) < Global.objectiveRad; // 5 elements
        (Event Player).isAfk != True; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Clear Status(Event Player, Phased Out); // 1 element
        Skip If(Array Contains(Global.objectivePlayerList, Event Player), True); // 4 elements
        Modify Global Variable(objectivePlayerList, Append To Array, Event Player); // 1 element
        //__label_if_27__:
        Wait(0.125, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        "biowasteDepositTimeDivisor: deposit-time divisor for ordinary biowaste, used to calculate this tick's progress."
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, 8.9); // 2 elements
        "Extra points if correct biowaste type"
        Skip If(Compare(First Of(Global.objectiveQueueSlot), !=, (Event Player).objectiveActorKindSlot), True); // 8 elements
        "biowasteDepositTimeDivisor: shorter deposit-time divisor for the requested resource, to award faster progress."
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, 2.9); // 2 elements
        //__label_if_28__:
        "Damage player if resource is blood, damage more if above health."
        Damage(Event Player, Event Player, If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.objectiveQueueSlot), ==, 3), If-Then-Else(Compare(Health(Event Player), >, 200), 50.33, 10.33), Null)); // 20 elements
        "(Amount per resource / dump time) x tick speed"
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, biomorphingResourceAmount, Subtract, 1.041666666666666); // 2 elements
        "(Amount per resource / dump time) x tick speed, if resource is blood then this will go slower x the number of players"
        Modify Global Variable(objectiveProgressSlot, Add, Multiply(Divide(25, If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.objectiveQueueSlot), ==, 3), Multiply(Number Of Players(Team 1), 4.167), Global.tmp0)), 0.125)); // 22 elements
        If(Compare(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, >=, Global.objectiveCheckpointSlot)); // 6 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveQueueSlot, Remove From Array By Index, False); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(objectiveProgressSlot, Global.objectiveCheckpointSlot); // 2 elements
            Set Global Variable(isBiomorphingGoalPending, True); // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveCheckpointSlot, Add, 25); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
        If(Compare((Event Player).biomorphingResourceAmount, <=, Null)); // 6 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Position Of(Event Player), 75); // 6 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion, Color(White), Add(Position Of(Event Player), Global.offsetUpQuarterConst), 8); // 9 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("  Biowaste deposited!")); // 5 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Modify Global Variable(objectivePlayerList, Remove From Array By Value, Event Player); // 1 element
    }
}

        Start Facing(Global.objectiveActorSlot, Up, 100, To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 3 elements
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Global.objectiveActorSlot, actorAbilitySlot1, True); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Global.objectiveActorSlot, actorTeamSlot3, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Global.objectiveActorSlot, actorMotionSlot1, Position Of(Global.objectiveActorSlot)); // 4 elements
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Start Forcing Player Position(Global.objectiveActorSlot, (Global.objectiveActorSlot).actorMotionSlot1, True); // 5 elements
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Global.objectiveActorSlot, actorMotionSlot1, Add(Position Of(Global.objectiveActorSlot), Multiply(100, Up)), 3, None); // 10 elements
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Global.objectiveActorSlot, actorMotionSlot1, Add(Position Of(Global.objectiveActorSlot), Multiply(100, Up)), 10, None); // 10 elements
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements

//107 elements
rule ("SET UP TERMINAL POSITIONS") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.phaseSlot10 == 7; // 3 elements
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        Global.isObjectiveComplete == False; // 2 elements
        Or(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectiveProgressSlot), !=, Count Of(Global.objectivePositionsSlot)), Is True For Any(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Compare(X Component Of(Current Array Element), ==, Null))) == True; // 17 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(False, Abort When False); // 1 element
        "satelliteTerminalCandidatePositionList: grid points 10-50m from the objective, to choose a terminal location with sufficient spacing."
        Set Global Variable(tmp0, Filtered Array(Global.mapGridList, And(Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.currentObjectivePosition), >, 10), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.currentObjectivePosition), <, 50)))); // 20 elements
        If(Compare(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, ==, Empty Array)); // 5 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectivePositionsSlot, Append To Array, Random Value In Array(Global.tmp0)); // 3 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            "satelliteSpacingTerminalIndex: existing terminal index, to exclude nearby candidate locations from satelliteTerminalCandidatePositionList."
            For Global Variable(tmp1, 0, Count Of(Global.objectivePositionsSlot), True); // 4 elements
                Modify Global Variable(tmp0, Remove From Array By Value, Filtered Array(Global.tmp0, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Global.tmp1)), <, Global.phaseSlot2))); // 14 elements
            End; // 1 element
            If(Compare(Global.tmp0, !=, Empty Array)); // 5 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectivePositionsSlot, Append To Array, Random Value In Array(Global.tmp0)); // 3 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(objectivePositionsSlot, Remove From Array By Index, Subtract(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionsSlot), True)); // 5 elements
                Modify Global Variable(phaseSlot2, Subtract, 5); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        If(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionsSlot), >, 3)); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(objectivePositionsSlot, Empty Array); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(objectiveProgressSlot, Array(Null, Null, Null)); // 5 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//412 elements
rule ("PLAYERS REPAIR TERMINALS") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.phaseSlot10 == 7; // 3 elements
        Global.isObjectiveComplete == False; // 2 elements
        Global.objectivePlayerList != Empty Array; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        "Check each terminal\nsatelliteRepairTerminalIndex: terminal index, to update its repair progress and announce its completion.\nsatelliteRepairPlayerCount: players within the current terminal's repair radius for this tick."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of(Global.objectivePositionsSlot), True); // 4 elements
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, Global.tmp0), <, 100)); // 9 elements
                Set Global Variable(tmp1, Count Of(Filtered Array(Global.objectivePlayerList, Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Global.tmp0), Current Array Element), <, Global.objectiveRad)))); // 15 elements
                If(Global.tmp1); // 2 elements
                    Skip If(Compare(Global.tmp1, <=, 1), True); // 6 elements
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(objectiveProgressSlot, Global.tmp0, Add, 0.4); // 3 elements
                    //__label_if_31__:
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(objectiveProgressSlot, Global.tmp0, Add, 1.1); // 3 elements
                    "If terminal is repaired"
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, Global.tmp0), >=, 100)); // 9 elements
                        Set Global Variable At Index(objectiveProgressSlot, Global.tmp0, 100); // 3 elements
                        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("A terminal is back online!")); // 7 elements
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion, Global.objectiveColor, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Global.tmp0), Multiply(Global.objectiveRad, 2)); // 12 elements
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion Sound, Global.objectiveColor, Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Global.tmp0), 100); // 9 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "All terminals repaired"
        If(Is True For All(Global.objectiveProgressSlot, Compare(Current Array Element, >=, 100))); // 8 elements
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("All terminals are back online!")); // 7 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion Sound, Global.objectiveColor, Global.currentObjectivePosition, 200); // 6 elements
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Connecting to satellite...")); // 7 elements
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Connection established!")); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(satelliteLaserPosition, Global.currentObjectivePosition); // 2 elements
            "Terminal connection beams\nsatelliteFiringTerminalIndex: terminal index, to connect each terminal to the satellite laser with a beam."
            For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of(Global.objectivePositionsSlot), True); // 4 elements
                Create Beam Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Beam, Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(700, Up)), Value In Array(Global.objectivePositionsSlot, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0)), Global.objectiveColor, Visible To Position and Radius); // 16 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Preparing to fire...")); // 7 elements
            Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Stand clear of the laser")); // 7 elements
            "Warning icon"
            Create Icon(All Players(All Teams), Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Up), Warning, Visible To and Position, Color(Sky Blue), True); // 7 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            "Laser distance"
            Create In-World Text(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Danger: {0}m", Distance Between(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Local Player)), Global.satelliteLaserPosition, True, Do Not Clip, Visible To Position and String, Color(Sky Blue), Default Visibility); // 12 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 1 element
            "Laser center beam"
            Create Beam Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Beam, Add(Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(700, Up)), Direction From Angles(Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, 360), 0)), Add(Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(-700, Up)), Direction From Angles(Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, 360), 0)), Color(Sky Blue), Visible To Position and Radius); // 32 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            "Laser center beam"
            Create Beam Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Beam, Add(Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(700, Up)), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, 360), 0), -1)), Add(Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(-700, Up)), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Multiply(Total Time Elapsed, 360), 0), -1)), Color(Sky Blue), Visible To Position and Radius); // 38 elements
            Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            "Sattellite firing light shafts\nsatelliteLaserShaftLayerIndex: laser light-shaft layer index, to place six shafts at heights -35 through 65."
            For Global Variable(tmp0, -1, 5, True); // 3 elements
                Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Light Shaft, Color(Aqua), Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(Add(-15, Multiply(20, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0))), Up)), True, Visible To Position and Radius); // 18 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Firing sequence commenced")); // 7 elements
            "Chase closest living zombie"
            Call Subroutine(chaseSatelliteTarget); // 1 element
            "Fire satellite"
            For Global Variable(phaseSlot1, 40, False, -1); // 3 elements
                Stop Chasing Global Variable(satelliteLaserPosition); // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(objectiveActorSlot, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), Not((Current Array Element).actorTeamSlot3)), Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.satelliteLaserPosition)))); // 14 elements
                Skip If(Compare(Global.objectiveActorSlot, ==, Null), True); // 5 elements
                "Chase closest living zombie"
                Call Subroutine(chaseSatelliteTarget); // 1 element
                //__label_if_32__:
                Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                "satelliteLaserImpactPosition: laser impact position on the ground, shared by explosion visuals and both damage checks."
                Set Global Variable(tmp0, Add(Ray Cast Hit Position(Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Multiply(2, Up)), Add(Global.satelliteLaserPosition, Global.offsetDownHundredConst), All Players(All Teams), Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Is On Ground(Current Array Element)), False), Multiply(0.1, Up))); // 28 elements
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Sky Blue), Global.tmp0, 5); // 6 elements
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion Sound, Color(Sky Blue), Global.tmp0, 200); // 6 elements
                "Damage Zombies"
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Global.tmp0, 5, Team 2, Off), Null, 500); // 9 elements
                "Damage Players"
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Global.tmp0, 5, Team 1, Off), Null, 70); // 9 elements
                If(Compare(Global.phaseSlot1, >, 5)); // 6 elements
                    "Give all bots quick respawn time"
                    Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 2), recoveryTimeSlot, 1); // 4 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    "Give all bots a long respawn time"
                    Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 2), recoveryTimeSlot, 12); // 4 elements
                    Set Global Variable(botSpawnTimeList, Mapped Array(Global.botSpawnTimeList, 12)); // 5 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Stop Chasing Global Variable(satelliteLaserPosition); // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(isObjectiveComplete, True); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//195 elements
rule ("PLAYER INITIALIZATION") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Game In Progress == True; // 1 element
        Global.isPregameSetup != True; // 2 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, initialMaxHealth, Max Health(Event Player)); // 2 elements
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).enhancementId, !=, 10), True); // 7 elements
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, initialMaxHealth, Divide, Min(2, Add(True, Multiply((Event Player).actorTeamSlot22, 0.005)))); // 11 elements
        //__label_if_33__:
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, invisibilityCount, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, 1); // 2 elements
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).augmentId, !=, 8), True); // 7 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState0, Null); // 1 element
        //__label_if_34__:
        Call Subroutine(applyScaledMoveSpeed); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isAttached, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isAfk, False); // 1 element
        Abort If(Compare((Event Player).isHeroSwitchRespawn, ==, True)); // 6 elements
        Disable Messages(Event Player); // 1 element
        Disable Game Mode In-World UI(Event Player); // 1 element
        Disable Game Mode HUD(Event Player); // 1 element
        Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Start Rule(setDifficulty, Restart Rule); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isReviving, False); // 1 element
        Disable Built-In Game Mode Respawning(Event Player); // 1 element
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isInfected, False); // 1 element
        Enable Death Spectate Target HUD(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, reviveDuration, 5); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, recoveryTimeSlot, (Event Player).reviveDuration); // 3 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Global.mustReviveTime); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, infectionLevel, Null); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(enableDisableAbilities); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, sizeScale, 1); // 2 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).pregameState, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
            If(Not(Array Contains(Global.userNameList, Custom String("{0}", Event Player)))); // 9 elements
                Call Subroutine(updateUsers); // 1 element
                Call Subroutine(teleportToClosestPlayer); // 1 element
                Set Status(Event Player, Null, Invincible, 10); // 2 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, isPlayerActive, True); // 1 element
            Else; // 1 element
                "Index of user in user array"
                Call Subroutine(resolveReconnectUserIndex); // 1 element
                "Read-only snapshot for this synchronous segment; do not retain it across kill()/wait()."
                Set Global Variable(tmp0, Value In Array(Global.playerStatsList, (Event Player).reconnectUserIndex)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Value In Array(Global.tmp0, True)); // 4 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, recoveryTimeSlot, Value In Array(Global.tmp0, 2)); // 5 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, reviveDuration, Value In Array(Global.tmp0, 3)); // 5 elements
                If(Compare(First Of(Global.tmp0), ==, Null)); // 6 elements
                    Call Subroutine(teleportToClosestPlayer); // 1 element
                    Set Status(Event Player, Null, Phased Out, 10); // 2 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, isPlayerActive, True); // 1 element
                Else If(Compare(First Of(Global.tmp0), >=, 1)); // 7 elements
                    Teleport(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.tmp0, 4)); // 5 elements
                    While(Is Alive(Event Player)); // 2 elements
                        Kill(Event Player, Null); // 1 element
                        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, isPlayerActive, True); // 1 element
                    Skip If(Compare(First Of(Value In Array(Global.playerStatsList, (Event Player).reconnectUserIndex)), !=, 2), True); // 11 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, 0.5); // 2 elements
                    //__label_if_35__:
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Wait Until(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), >=, 1), 5); // 8 elements
            If(Global.isSpecialGameMode); // 2 elements
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                Teleport(Event Player, Random Value In Array(Filtered Array(Global.mapGridList, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.walkCenter), <, 15)))); // 12 elements
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Set Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Global.currentObjectivePosition), To World); // 5 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isPlayerActive, True); // 1 element
            Cancel Primary Action(Event Player); // 1 element
            Clear Status(Event Player, Invincible); // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(updateUsers); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "Release the pregame camera only after placement and facing, as in the original transition."
        Stop Camera(Event Player); // 1 element
        Clear Status(Event Player, Rooted); // 1 element
        Set Invisible(Event Player, None); // 1 element
    }
}

//288 elements
rule ("EFFECTS/TEXTS") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;

        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Game In Progress == True; // 1 element
        (Event Player).isPlayerActive == True; // 3 elements

        Global.isPregameSetup != True; // 2 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).isHeroSwitchRespawn == False; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        "Infected aura"

//61 elements
rule ("PLAYER GETS DOWNED") {
    event {
        Player Died;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).isPlayerActive == True; // 3 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, recoveryTimeSlot, Divide((Event Player).reviveDuration, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.challengeSetting, ==, 2), 2, 1))); // 15 elements
        Stop All Damage Over Time(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, infectionLevel, Null); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isInfected, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 2), Compare((Current Array Element).actorTeamSlot5, ==, Event Player)), actorTeamSlot5, Null); // 10 elements
        Modify Player Variable(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Array Contains((Current Array Element).candidateTargetList, Event Player)), candidateTargetList, Remove From Array By Value, Event Player); // 9 elements
        If(Event Was Environment); // 1 element
            If(And(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Reinhardt)), Compare(Event Ability, ==, Button(Ability 1)))); // 12 elements
                Call Subroutine(teleportToClosestPlayer); // 1 element
            Else; // 1 element
                Call Subroutine(teleportDownedPlayerToWalkable); // 1 element
    }
}

//265 elements
rule ("REVIVE PLAYER OR ADVANCE DEATH TIMER") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dead(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).isPlayerActive == True; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).rescueTimeRemaining > Null; // 3 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        "Save row in ReconnectField order."
        Set Global Variable At Index(playerStatsList, (Event Player).reconnectUserIndex, Array(1, (Event Player).rescueTimeRemaining, (Event Player).recoveryTimeSlot, (Event Player).reviveDuration, Position Of(Event Player))); // 17 elements
        Wait Until(And(Not((Event Player).isReviving), Is True For Any(Filtered Array(Remove From Array(Global.playerTargetList, Event Player), Not((Current Array Element).invisibilityCount)), Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player), <, 5))), 0.5); // 23 elements
        If(Is True For Any(Filtered Array(Remove From Array(Global.playerTargetList, Event Player), Not((Current Array Element).invisibilityCount)), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 5))); // 17 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isReviving, True); // 1 element
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion, Color(Yellow), Position Of(Event Player), 10); // 6 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, recoveryTimeSlot, Add, -0.5); // 2 elements
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).rescueTimeRemaining, >=, 70), True); // 7 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Add, 0.5); // 2 elements
            //__label_if_36__:
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isReviving, False); // 1 element
            "If dome will pass player before their death timer runs out, set the death timer to how long it will take for the dome to reach them"
            If(And(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), ==, 1), Compare(Subtract(Global.extractionZoneRad, Distance Between(Event Player, Global.currentObjectivePosition)), <, (Event Player).rescueTimeRemaining))); // 20 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Divide(Subtract(Global.extractionZoneRad, Distance Between(Event Player, Global.currentObjectivePosition)), 2.5)); // 10 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Add, -0.5); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            "Player is dead outside of extraction zone"
            If(And(Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), ==, 1), Compare(Distance Between(Global.currentObjectivePosition, Event Player), >, Global.extractionZoneRad))); // 16 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Null); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Skip If(Compare(Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Y Component Of(Nearest Walkable Position(Position Of(Event Player)))), >=, -0.1), True); // 12 elements
            Call Subroutine(teleportDownedPlayerToWalkable); // 1 element
            //__label_if_37__:
        End; // 1 element
        If(Compare((Event Player).recoveryTimeSlot, <=, Null)); // 6 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, reviveDuration, Add, True); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, recoveryTimeSlot, (Event Player).reviveDuration); // 3 elements
            Resurrect(Event Player); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isReviving, False); // 1 element
            Set Status(Event Player, Null, Phased Out, 5); // 2 elements
            Wait Until(Is Alive(Event Player), 2); // 3 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).rescueTimeRemaining, <=, Null)); // 6 elements
            "Save row in ReconnectField order."
            Set Global Variable At Index(playerStatsList, (Event Player).reconnectUserIndex, Array(2, (Event Player).rescueTimeRemaining, (Event Player).recoveryTimeSlot, (Event Player).reviveDuration, Global.offsetDownHundredConst)); // 17 elements
            Big Message(Remove From Array(All Players(All Teams), Event Player), Custom String("{0} {1} died!", Event Player, Hero Icon String(Hero Of(Event Player)))); // 11 elements
            If(And(Compare(Global.mustReviveTime, ==, 60), Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), !=, 1))); // 14 elements
                Big Message(Remove From Array(All Players(All Teams), Event Player), Custom String("Save {0} by finishing the objective!", Event Player)); // 9 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(mustReviveTime, Subtract, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.mustReviveTime, <=, 30), Null, 10)); // 10 elements
            Teleport(Event Player, Global.offsetDownHundredConst); // 2 elements
            Set Invisible(Event Player, All); // 1 element
            Wait Until(Global.isObjectiveComplete, 999999999999); // 3 elements
            Resurrect(Event Player); // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(teleportToClosestPlayer); // 1 element
            Wait Until(Is Alive(Event Player), 999999999999); // 3 elements
            Set Invisible(Event Player, None); // 1 element
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Impact Sound, Null, Event Player, 100); // 4 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, isReviving, False); // 1 element
        If(Is Alive(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, rescueTimeRemaining, Global.mustReviveTime); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(saveAlivePlayerState); // 1 element
    }
}

//118 elements
rule ("PLAYER PICKS UP HEALTH PACK") {
    event {
        Player Received Healing;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Event Was Health Pack == True; // 1 element
    }
    actions {
        Start Heal Over Time(Event Player, Null, 5, 20); // 3 elements
        "Self-attributed packs already charge Overcharge through playerDealtHealing."
        If(And(And(Global.isGameplayActive, Compare((Event Player).enhancementId, ==, 9)), Compare(Healer, !=, Event Player))); // 15 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot22, Add, Divide(Event Healing, 3)); // 4 elements
        End; // 1 element
        If(Or(Compare(Global.difficultySetting, <, 3), Compare(Match Time, >, Global.matchEndTime))); // 12 elements
            "Update known health pack positions"
            If(Is True For All(Global.healthpackPositionList, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player), >, 5))); // 10 elements
                Modify Global Variable(healthpackPositionList, Append To Array, Position Of(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Stop All Damage Over Time(Event Player); // 1 element
            Clear Status(Event Player, Burning); // 1 element
            If(Compare((Event Player).infectionLevel, >, Null)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, isInfected, False); // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, infectionLevel, Null); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        Else If(And((Event Player).isInfected, Compare((Event Player).augmentId, !=, 8))); // 11 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Match time has reached 0. Health packs no longer cure infection.")); // 5 elements
        End; // 1 element
        If(Compare((Event Player).augmentId, ==, 8)); // 7 elements
            "Only reduce existing infection; never create or increase vaccine reserves.\nScripted healing is not a health-pack event, so this cannot recurse."
            Heal(Event Player, Null, Event Healing); // 1 element
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).infectionLevel, <=, Null), True); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, infectionLevel, Max(False, Subtract((Event Player).infectionLevel, True))); // 7 elements
            //__label_if_38__:
            Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), 7)); // 5 elements
            "Refresh duration without stacking; preserve other movement multipliers."
            If(Compare((Event Player).augmentState0, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, Multiply, 1.5); // 2 elements
                Call Subroutine(applyScaledMoveSpeed); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState0, Add(Total Time Elapsed, 5)); // 4 elements
    }
}

//89 elements
rule ("PLAYER AFK") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isPregameSetup != True; // 2 elements
        Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("General"), Custom String("Allow players to go AFK"), True, 3) == True; // 14 elements
        (Event Player).isPlayerActive == True; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).isAttached != True; // 3 elements
        Magnitude Of(Throttle Of(Event Player)) == False; // 3 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)) == False; // 4 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)) == False; // 4 elements
        Array Contains(Global.objectivePlayerList, Event Player) == False; // 4 elements
        Is Communicating Any Voice line(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Not((Event Player).isAfk)); // 4 elements
            Wait(45, Abort When False); // 2 elements
            Small Message(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Is Alive(Event Player)), Custom String("{0} {1} is afk", Event Player, Hero Icon String(Hero Of(Event Player)))); // 12 elements
            Wait Until(Is Alive(Event Player), 999999999999); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isAfk, True); // 1 element
            Set Invisible(Event Player, All); // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(playerTargetList, Remove From Array By Value, Event Player); // 1 element
            Set Status(Event Player, Null, Phased Out, 999999999999); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
        If((Event Player).isAfk); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, isAfk, False); // 1 element
            Set Invisible(Event Player, None); // 1 element
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("{0} {1} is back!", Event Player, Hero Icon String(Hero Of(Event Player)))); // 9 elements
            Clear Status(Event Player, Phased Out); // 1 element
    }
}

//123 elements
rule ("OBJECTIVE CHANGES AND PLAYER ENTITIES UPDATE") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Or(Compare((Event Player).objectiveTypeSnapshot, !=, Global.objectiveValue), Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), ==, 1)) == True; // 15 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Or(Compare((Event Player).objectiveTypeSnapshot, !=, Global.objectiveValue), Compare(Count Of(Global.objectivePositionList), ==, 1))); // 15 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, objectiveTypeSnapshot, Global.phaseSlot10); // 2 elements
            If(Compare(Team Of(Event Player), ==, Team 1)); // 6 elements
                "Destroy all current objective effects and texts"
                For Player Variable(Event Player, visualCleanupIndex, 0, Count Of((Event Player).visualEntityIdList), True); // 5 elements
                    Destroy Icon(Value In Array((Event Player).visualEntityIdList, (Event Player).visualCleanupIndex)); // 7 elements
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, visualEntityIdList, Empty Array); // 1 element
            Else; // 1 element
                "Destroy all current objective effects and texts"
                For Player Variable(Event Player, visualCleanupIndex, 0, Count Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot28), True); // 5 elements
                    Destroy Icon(Value In Array((Event Player).actorTeamSlot28, (Event Player).visualCleanupIndex)); // 7 elements
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot28, Empty Array); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            If(Compare((Event Player).objectiveTypeSnapshot, ==, 3)); // 7 elements
                "Player carrying resources icon"
                Create Icon(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), And(And(Is Alive(Event Player), Compare(Current Array Element, !=, Event Player)), Compare((Event Player).biomorphingResourceAmount, >, Null))), Event Player, Recycle, Visible To Position and Color, If-Then-Else(Compare(Team Of(Event Player), ==, Team 1), Value In Array(Global.phaseSlot4, (Event Player).objectiveActorKindSlot), Color(White)), True); // 32 elements
                If(Compare(Team Of(Event Player), ==, Team 1)); // 6 elements
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, visualEntityIdList, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                Else; // 1 element
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot28, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
    }
}

//8 elements
rule ("CASSIDY GRENADE HACK") {
    event {
        Player Dealt Damage;
        Team 1;
        Cassidy;

//219 elements
rule ("BOT DIES") {
    event {
        Player Died;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Game In Progress == True; // 1 element
        Is Dead(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player) == False; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(And(And((Event Player).actorAbilitySlot1, Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Anran))), Not(Event Was Environment))); // 13 elements
            Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
            Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ultimate)); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorAbilitySlot1, Null); // 1 element
            Wait(Random Integer(True, 4), Ignore Condition); // 4 elements
            Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ultimate)); // 2 elements
            Abort; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        If(Array Contains(Global.objectiveQueueSlot, Event Player)); // 4 elements
            If(Or(Compare(Random Integer(150, (Event Player).botBaseHealth), >, 175), Compare(Global.difficultySetting, <=, 3))); // 17 elements
                Modify Global Variable(objectiveProgressSlot, Add, If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).botBaseHealth, >, 500), 10, Multiply((Event Player).botBaseHealth, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.difficultySetting, <=, 2), 0.04, 0.035)))); // 25 elements
                Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String(" {0}  Kill Bonus", Icon String(Plus))); // 8 elements
                "Miniboss kill bonus"
                If(Array Contains(Global.miniBossList, Event Player)); // 4 elements
                    Modify Global Variable(objectiveProgressSlot, Add, 10); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Null, Global.currentObjectivePosition, 100); // 5 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Array(Hero(Symmetra), Hero(Ana)), Hero Of(Event Player)), 3, Null), Ignore Condition); // 14 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot5, Null); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, candidateTargetList, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Stop All Damage Over Time(Event Player); // 1 element
        Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player); // 1 element
        Stop Facing(Event Player); // 1 element
        If(Or(Not(Event Was Environment), Compare(Team Of(Attacker), ==, Team 1))); // 9 elements
            "Remove miniboss from zombie"
            Set Global Variable At Index(miniBossList, Index Of Array Value(Global.miniBossList, Event Player), Null); // 4 elements
            Disable Nameplates(Event Player, All Players(All Teams)); // 3 elements
            "Allow for custom revive times"
            Wait Until(Compare((Event Player).recoveryTimeSlot, >, Null), 0.125); // 7 elements
            Skip If((Event Player).recoveryTimeSlot, True); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, recoveryTimeSlot, Add(True, Divide(Add(Match Time, Global.matchEndTime), 60))); // 9 elements
            //__label_if_43__:
            Skip If(Compare(Count Of(Global.botSpawnTimeList), >=, Global.numberOfBots), True); // 7 elements
            Modify Global Variable(botSpawnTimeList, Append To Array, (Event Player).recoveryTimeSlot); // 3 elements
            //__label_if_44__:
            If((Event Player).actorTeamSlot20); // 3 elements
                Modify Global Variable(customTeleportPositionList, Append To Array, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot20); // 3 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Wait Until(Compare((Event Player).damageOverTimeRemaining, <=, Null), 10); // 7 elements
            Call Subroutine(updateBotSpawnPoints); // 1 element
            Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Slot Of(Event Player)); // 3 elements
            Abort; // 1 element
        Else If(And(Event Was Environment, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Vector(X Component Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Z Component Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11))), <, 10))); // 20 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot7, False); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot3, True); // 1 element
        Teleport(Event Player, Vector(500, 50, 500)); // 7 elements
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Resurrect(Event Player); // 1 element
    }
}

        Magnitude Of(Throttle Of(Event Player)) != False; // 3 elements
        Or(Is On Ground(Event Player), And(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player), Compare(Altitude Of(Event Player), <, 10))) == True; // 14 elements

        "Makes sure bot is moving forward"
        Speed Of In Direction(Event Player, World Vector Of(Throttle Of(Event Player), Event Player, Rotation)) >= Null; // 7 elements
        Is In Line of Sight(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Multiply(Global.offsetUpThreeQuartersConst, (Event Player).sizeScale)), Add(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Multiply(Global.offsetUpThreeQuartersConst, (Event Player).sizeScale)), Multiply(Multiply(Normalize(World Vector Of(Throttle Of(Event Player), Event Player, Rotation)), 3), (Event Player).sizeScale)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS) == True; // 34 elements
    }

        Wait(0.125, Abort When False); // 2 elements

            Wait(2, Restart When True); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element

        Skip If(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player), True); // 4 elements
        Wait(5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        //__label_if_48__:
    }

//74 elements
rule ("enterExitTeleport") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        enterExitTeleport;
    }
    actions {
        If((Event Player).actorTeamSlot3); // 3 elements
            Set Invisible(Event Player, All); // 1 element
            Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player); // 1 element
            Set Gravity(Event Player, False); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Status(Event Player, Null, Rooted, 0.25); // 2 elements
            Set Invisible(Event Player, None); // 1 element
            Set Gravity(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
                If(Array Contains(Global.botSpawnPointList, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot11)); // 6 elements
                    Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.botSpawnWaypointList, Index Of Array Value(Global.botSpawnPointList, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot11))), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, To World, Replace existing throttle, None); // 13 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction From Angles(Add(Random Integer(-90, 90), Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Event Player, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element)))))), 0), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, To World, Replace existing throttle, None); // 25 elements
                End; // 1 element
            Else If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, !=, Null)); // 6 elements
                Start Rule(restoreDefaultFacingMovement, Do Nothing); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }

//293 elements
rule ("teleportBots") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        teleportBots;
    }
    actions {
        Abort If(And(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Anran)), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player))); // 9 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot3, True); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(updateBotSpawnPoints); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(enterExitTeleport); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot7, False); // 1 element
        Wait Until(And(Not((Event Player).actorTeamSlot18), (Event Player).actorTeamSlot26), 999999999999); // 9 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot20, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
            While((Event Player).actorTeamSlot3); // 3 elements
                "Standoff, Extraction, Biomorphing, Zombie Egg"
                If(Array Contains(Array(1, 3, 6), Global.phaseSlot10)); // 11 elements
                    Set Global Variable(tmp1, Global.currentObjectivePosition); // 2 elements
                    Call Subroutine(setClosestPlayerTeleportCenter); // 1 element
                "Boss"
                Else If(Compare(Global.phaseSlot10, ==, 2)); // 6 elements
                    Set Global Variable(tmp1, Position Of(First Of(Global.bossStateList))); // 4 elements
                    Call Subroutine(setClosestPlayerTeleportCenter); // 1 element
                "Survivor Rescue"
                Else If(Compare(Global.phaseSlot10, ==, 4)); // 6 elements
                    If(Compare((Global.objectiveActorSlot).rescueLeader, ==, Null)); // 7 elements
                        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Position Of(Global.objectiveActorSlot)); // 3 elements
                        Call Subroutine(setClosestPlayerTeleportCenter); // 1 element
                    Else; // 1 element
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot0, Position Of((Global.objectiveActorSlot).rescueLeader)); // 5 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                "Killzone"
                Else If(Compare(Global.phaseSlot10, ==, 5)); // 6 elements
                    "If players are in the killzone"
                    If(Is True For Any(Global.playerTargetList, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Global.objectiveMotionSlot), <, Global.objectiveRad))); // 11 elements
                        "Player closeset to the center of the killzone"
                        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Global.objectiveMotionSlot); // 2 elements
                        Call Subroutine(setClosestPlayerTeleportCenter); // 1 element
                    Else; // 1 element
                        "Average player positon"
                        Call Subroutine(locateAveragePlayerLocation); // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                "Satellite Uplink"
                Else If(Compare(Global.phaseSlot10, ==, 7)); // 6 elements
                    "Checks if any players are currently repairing terminals"
                    If(Compare(Global.objectivePlayerList, !=, Empty Array)); // 5 elements
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot0, Position Of(Random Value In Array(Global.objectivePlayerList))); // 4 elements
                    Else; // 1 element
                        "Closest player to sattelite firing position"
                        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Global.currentObjectivePosition); // 2 elements
                        Call Subroutine(setClosestPlayerTeleportCenter); // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                "Default"
                Else; // 1 element
                    Call Subroutine(locateAveragePlayerLocation); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                "The search has no empty-target guard and may continue using a stale or zero center.\nThe shared average loop preserves this behavior; guard the search separately when fixing it."
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot11, Null); // 1 element
                "Teleport with max distance constraint no view angle constraint"
                While(Or(Or(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11, ==, Null), Is True For Any(Global.playerTargetList, Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11, Current Array Element), <, 15))), Is True For All(Global.playerTargetList, Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11, Current Array Element), >, 40)))); // 32 elements
                    "teleportCandidateSpawnPointList: saved spawn points within teleport range, to choose a nearby destination."
                    Set Global Variable(tmp0, Filtered Array(Global.botSpawnPointList, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot0), <, 40))); // 12 elements
                    "Filter out spawn points on cooldown\nteleportCandidateSpawnPointList: nearby spawn points whose cooldown permits use, to choose a ready destination."
                    Set Global Variable(tmp0, Filtered Array(Global.tmp0, Compare(Value In Array(Global.botSpawnPointTimerList, Index Of Array Value(Global.botSpawnPointList, Current Array Element)), <, 15))); // 14 elements
                    "Bots utilize saved spawn points"
                    If(And(Compare(Global.botSpawnPointList, !=, Empty Array), Compare(Global.tmp0, !=, Empty Array))); // 11 elements
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot11, Random Value In Array(Global.tmp0)); // 3 elements
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(botSpawnPointTimerList, Index Of Array Value(Global.botSpawnPointList, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot11), Add, 5); // 7 elements
                        Break; // 1 element
                    Else; // 1 element
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot11, Nearest Walkable Position(Add((Event Player).actorTeamSlot0, Multiply(Direction From Angles(Random Real(0, 360), Random Real(-20, 10)), Random Real(0, 40))))); // 22 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                    Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
                If(And(Is True For All(Global.playerTargetList, Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11, Current Array Element), >, 10)), Is True For All(Filtered Array(Global.playerTargetList, Is In View Angle(Current Array Element, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot11, 55)), Not(Is In Line of Sight(Eye Position(Current Array Element), Add((Event Player).actorTeamSlot11, Global.losOffsetConst), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))))); // 35 elements
                    Teleport(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot11); // 3 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot3, False); // 1 element
                "Teleport was unsuccessful due to line of sight and view angle"
                Else If(Array Contains(Global.botSpawnPointList, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot11)); // 6 elements
                    Call Subroutine(removeBotSpawnPoint); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Teleport(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot20); // 3 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot20, Null); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot3, False); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(enterExitTeleport); // 1 element
    }
}

//39 elements
rule ("locateAveragePlayerLocation") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        locateAveragePlayerLocation;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot0, Add(Left, Right)); // 3 elements
        "Snapshot the selected players once; consume the list synchronously before any wait or call."
        If(And(Compare(Global.objectivePlayerList, ==, Empty Array), Is True For Any(Global.playerTargetList, Magnitude Of(Throttle Of(Current Array Element))))); // 12 elements
            Set Global Variable(tmp1, Filtered Array(Global.playerTargetList, Magnitude Of(Throttle Of(Current Array Element)))); // 6 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(tmp1, Global.playerTargetList); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "Preserve player order and the original loop counter as the divisor, including an empty list."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of(Global.tmp1), True); // 4 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot0, Add, Position Of(Value In Array(Global.tmp1, Global.tmp0))); // 6 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "Pre-existing empty-list behavior: the divisor is 0, and Workshop Divide returns 0, producing a zero center.\nAn early return here alone would not stop the caller's teleport search; this needs a separate fix."
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot0, Divide, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
    }
}

        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot22), True); // 5 elements
            Skip If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).actorTeamSlot23, Global.tmp0), >=, Multiply(6, (Event Player).sizeScale)), True); // 14 elements

            "Capture this cloud's position and post-growth radius; consume both before the tick wait.\nVisual effects keep reading the original arrays so they continue to reevaluate normally."

        While(And(And(Is Alive(Event Player), Not((Event Player).isCrowdControlled)), Compare(Distance Between(Eye Position(Event Player), Ray Cast Hit Position(Eye Position(Event Player), (Event Player).actorAbilitySlot1, All Living Players(Team 1), All Players(All Teams), True)), >, Distance Between(Eye Position(Event Player), (Event Player).actorMotionSlot1)))); // 32 elements

                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot10, First Of(Filtered Array(Global.playerTargetList, And(And(Not(Has Status(Current Array Element, Phased Out)), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).actorMotionSlot1, Eye Position(Current Array Element)), <, 1)), Is In Line of Sight((Event Player).actorMotionSlot1, Eye Position(Event Player), Enemy Barriers Block LOS))))); // 27 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorAbilitySlot1, Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10)); // 4 elements
                Break; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(0.032, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Lime Green), (Event Player).actorMotionSlot1, 0.25); // 7 elements
        "Tentacle retract"
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1, If-Then-Else(Is In Air(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Multiply(Global.offsetUpHalfConst, (Event Player).sizeScale)), Eye Position(Event Player)), 50, Destination and Rate); // 15 elements
        If(And(And(Is Alive(Event Player), Not((Event Player).isCrowdControlled)), Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, !=, Null))); // 14 elements
            Skip If(Compare(Absolute Value(Angle Difference(Horizontal Facing Angle Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10), Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot10)))), >=, 75), True); // 16 elements
            Set Status((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, Null, Knocked Down, 0.35); // 4 elements
            //__label_if_56__:
            Set Gravity((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, False); // 3 elements
            Apply Impulse((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, Direction Towards(Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10), Eye Position(Event Player)), Multiply((Event Player).sizeScale, If-Then-Else(Compare(Max Health((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10), <, 400), 15, 10)), To World, Cancel Contrary Motion); // 25 elements
            Wait(0.35, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Set Gravity((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, 100); // 4 elements
        End; // 1 element
        While(Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).actorMotionSlot1, If-Then-Else(Is In Air(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Multiply(Global.offsetUpHalfConst, (Event Player).sizeScale)), Eye Position(Event Player))), >, 0.25)); // 22 elements
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorAbilitySlot1, Null); // 1 element
        Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1); // 1 element
        "Lower cooldown for ulting boss if they do not hit a target"
        If(And(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player))); // 7 elements
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(restoreBotThrottle); // 1 element
            Wait(If-Then-Else(Is In Air((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5), 2.25, 4.5), Ignore Condition); // 9 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//189 elements
rule ("PRIME SLIMER (EMRE) FLIGHT (BOSS)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        Emre;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5 != Null; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).actorAbilitySlot1 == Null; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).actorTeamSlot15 != True; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).isCrowdControlled != True; // 3 elements
        Is On Ground(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player) == True; // 4 elements
        Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5) < 15; // 6 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Not(Has Status(Event Player, Hacked)), 1); // 5 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot15, True); // 1 element
        While(Not(Is Jumping(Event Player))); // 3 elements
            Call Subroutine(reenableBotWallAndJump); // 1 element
            Wait Until(Is Jumping(Event Player), 1); // 3 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Apply Impulse(Event Player, Up, Multiply(Random Real(6, 10), (Event Player).sizeScale), To World, Cancel Contrary Motion); // 9 elements
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Wait Until(Compare(Y Component Of(Velocity Of(Event Player)), <, Null), 3); // 7 elements
        Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ultimate)); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player), 1); // 3 elements
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Pickup Effect, Color(Black), Event Player, Multiply(5, (Event Player).sizeScale)); // 9 elements
        While(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot15, True); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot14, True); // 1 element
            Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player); // 1 element
            While(And(And(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5, !=, Null), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Not((Event Player).isCrowdControlled))); // 14 elements
                Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5), Evaluate Once(Direction From Angles(Random Real(0, 360), Random Real(-90, 90))))), If-Then-Else((Event Player).isCrowdControlled, Null, 200), To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 27 elements
                Press Button(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)); // 2 elements
                Skip If(Compare(Altitude Of(Event Player), >=, 3), True); // 6 elements
                Call Subroutine(reenableBotWallAndJump); // 1 element
                //__label_if_57__:
                "Entities that will be floated"
                Call Subroutine(entityFloat); // 1 element
                "slimerFlightTargetIndex: floated-player index, to lift low targets and push higher targets away during flight."
                For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of((Event Player).abilityTargetList), True); // 5 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot10, Value In Array((Event Player).abilityTargetList, Global.tmp0)); // 6 elements
                    If(Compare(Y Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10)), <, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)))); // 10 elements
                        "Make target float up"
                        Apply Impulse((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, Event Player)), -75), 0.75, To World, Incorporate Contrary Motion); // 12 elements
                    Else; // 1 element
                        "Push target away"
                        Apply Impulse((Event Player).actorTeamSlot10, Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot10)), -20), 0.75, To World, Incorporate Contrary Motion); // 12 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Wait(0.032, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(resumeBotMovementAndFacing); // 1 element
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(restoreBotThrottle); // 1 element
        Wait(10, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//179 elements
rule ("STALKER (MOIRA) FADE") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        Moira;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5 != Null; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).isCrowdControlled != True; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).actorTeamSlot13 == True; // 3 elements
        Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)) == False; // 4 elements
        Angle Between Vectors(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5))) < 45; // 11 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot14, True); // 1 element
        Disable Movement Collision With Players(Event Player); // 1 element
        Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), If-Then-Else(Or((Event Player).isCrowdControlled, Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Null, If-Then-Else(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player), 75, 1500)), To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 21 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot15, True); // 1 element
        Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Facing Direction Of(Event Player), (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot, To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude); // 4 elements
        For Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot4, 0, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player), 5, 3), True); // 10 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot9, False); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot13, True); // 1 element
            Enable Movement Collision With Environment(Event Player); // 1 element
            "Final fade"
            If(And(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot4, >=, 2), Not(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player)))); // 13 elements
                Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21)), If-Then-Else((Event Player).isCrowdControlled, Null, 100), To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 13 elements
                Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), <, 3), Is Using Ability 1(Event Player)), Null, (Event Player).aiThrottleSpeedSlot), To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude); // 21 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, Multiply, 2); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(applyScaledMoveSpeed); // 1 element
            Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)); // 2 elements
            Wait(0.125, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Wait Until(Not(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player)), 2); // 4 elements
            Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), False); // 2 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, movementSpeedScale, Divide, 2); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(applyScaledMoveSpeed); // 1 element
            If(Or(Or(Is Dead(Event Player), (Event Player).isCrowdControlled), Has Status(Event Player, Hacked))); // 10 elements
                Break; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            If(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player)); // 4 elements
                Wait(0.125, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
                Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ultimate)); // 2 elements
                Set Status(Event Player, Null, Rooted, True); // 1 element
                Wait Until(Not(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), 1.3); // 4 elements
                Cancel Primary Action(Event Player); // 1 element
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot9, True); // 1 element
                Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(resumeBotMovementAndFacing); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot9, True); // 1 element
        Enable Movement Collision With Players(Event Player); // 1 element
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player), 10, 5)); // 10 elements
    }
}

rule ("BLASTER (PHARAH) CHARGES ROCKET THEN FIRES") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        Pharah;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5 != Null; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).isCrowdControlled != True; // 3 elements
        Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)) == False; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Call Subroutine(beginBotOverride); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(startBlasterDaytakerFacing); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1, Null); // 1 element
        Set Damage Received(Event Player, 50); // 2 elements
        "charge up attack"
        Chase Player Variable Over Time(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1, 2, If-Then-Else(Is In Air((Event Player).actorTeamSlot5), 1, 2), None); // 10 elements
        Wait Until(Or(Is Dead(Event Player), Compare((Event Player).actorMotionSlot1, >=, 2)), 999999999999); // 11 elements
        Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1); // 1 element
        If(Is Dead(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            Set Damage Received(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1, Null); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot15, False); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot14, False); // 1 element
            Abort; // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            "charge up attack"
            Chase Player Variable Over Time(Event Player, actorMotionSlot1, False, 0.25, None); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Damage Received(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Or(Is Dead(Event Player), Not((Event Player).actorMotionSlot1)), 999999999999); // 8 elements
        If(Is Dead(Event Player)); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot15, False); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot14, False); // 1 element
            Abort; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        If(And(Is In Line of Sight(Position Of(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), World Vector Of(Vector(False, 11, 5), Event Player, Rotation)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS), Is In Line of Sight(Eye Position((Event Player).actorTeamSlot21), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), World Vector Of(Vector(False, 11, 5), Event Player, Rotation)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))); // 35 elements
            Wait Until(Not((Event Player).isCrowdControlled), 999999999999); // 5 elements
            Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Forward, 1, To Player, Replace existing throttle, None); // 2 elements
            Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)); // 2 elements
            Set Ammo(Event Player, False, Max Ammo(Event Player, False)); // 3 elements
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Wait Until(Compare(Vertical Speed Of(Event Player), <=, Null), 999999999999); // 6 elements
            Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player); // 1 element
            "Cooldown after flying"
            Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), 2); // 3 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            "Cooldown after staying on ground"
            Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), True); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        If(Array Contains(Global.bossStateList, Event Player)); // 4 elements
            While(And(Not(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Is Alive(Event Player))); // 6 elements
                Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
                Set Projectile Speed(Event Player, 35); // 2 elements
                Press Button(Event Player, Button(Ultimate)); // 2 elements
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Wait Until(Not(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), 5); // 4 elements
            Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), 4); // 3 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Primary Fire Enabled(Event Player, True); // 1 element
            Press Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(resumeBotMovementAndFacing); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(startBotThrottleAtTarget); // 1 element
    }
}

        (Event Player).actorTeamSlot5 != Null; // 3 elements
