//181 elements
rule ("extendAugmentMelee") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        extendAugmentMelee;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(tmp2, Filtered Array(Players Within Radius(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.tmp0, True), (Event Player).sizeScale), 2), Team 2, Surfaces), And(And(And(Is Alive(Current Array Element), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), >, Multiply(First Of(Global.tmp0), (Event Player).sizeScale))), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, Multiply(Value In Array(Global.tmp0, True), (Event Player).sizeScale))), Is In View Angle(Event Player, Eye Position(Current Array Element), Value In Array(Global.tmp0, 2))))); // 57 elements
        "The caller's hero snapshot is no longer read after the windup; reuse its\nowner-only slot as a synchronous index. Each Damage receives one player."
        For Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState1, 0, Count Of(Global.tmp2), True); // 4 elements
            "Reuse the dead native-radius entry, preserving the other query metadata."
            Set Global Variable At Index(tmp0, False, Health(Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1))); // 7 elements
            Damage(Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1), Event Player, Global.tmp1); // 7 elements
            "Script Damage has no Event Ability, so the native-hit Pyrotechnic rule\ndeliberately ignores it. Queue only hits which actually removed health;\nthe existing burn worker keeps its stacking/cap/boss behavior and cannot recurse."
            If(Compare(Health(Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1)), <, First Of(Global.tmp0))); // 12 elements
                If(And(Compare((Event Player).enhancementId, ==, 3), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1)), <, 15))); // 20 elements
                    Set Status(Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1), Event Player, Burning, 3); // 7 elements
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot22, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1)); // 6 elements
                Else If(And(Compare((Event Player).enhancementId, ==, 9), Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot23, >, Null))); // 14 elements
                    Skip If(Compare((Event Player).actorTeamSlot24, !=, Null), True); // 6 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot24, Add(Total Time Elapsed, 0.3)); // 4 elements
                    //__label_if_79__:
                    Skip If(Compare(Total Time Elapsed, >=, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot24), True); // 6 elements
                    Damage(Value In Array(Global.tmp2, (Event Player).augmentState1), Event Player, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot23); // 8 elements
                    //__label_if_80__:
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Skip If(Compare(Global.tmp2, ==, Empty Array), True); // 5 elements
        "One impact per swing, on the nearest hit target. Rounded from 0.3 to 0.4\n(about one third larger); Grapnel Launcher uses the same impact size."
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Orange), Add(Position Of(First Of(Sorted Array(Global.tmp2, Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element)))), Up), 0.4); // 14 elements
        //__label_if_81__:
    }
}

//203 elements
rule ("AMMO PACK MAGAZINES") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).augmentId == 5; // 4 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        "Cache once per hero/weapon, so respawns and returning to an earlier loadout\ncannot repeatedly double an already enlarged magazine. No polling loop.\nDisarm the previous loadout's refill snapshot during the initialization wait."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, Null); // 1 element
        Skip If((Event Player).augmentState0, True); // 3 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState0, Empty Array); // 1 element
        //__label_if_82__:
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState1, First Of(Filtered Array((Event Player).augmentState0, And(Compare(First Of(Current Array Element), ==, Hero Of(Event Player)), Compare(Value In Array(Current Array Element, True), ==, Weapon(Event Player)))))); // 19 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).augmentState1, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState1, Array(Hero Of(Event Player), Weapon(Event Player), Multiply(Max Ammo(Event Player, False), 2), Multiply(Max Ammo(Event Player, True), 2))); // 18 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState0, Append To Array, Array((Event Player).augmentState1)); // 5 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "Only write real magazines with a readable, positive capacity. In particular,\na single-magazine weapon must not receive a zero write to the unused clip 1.\nScratch inputs live only through this synchronous loop, not the wait below."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, 2, True); // 3 elements
            Set Global Variable(tmp1, Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, Add(Global.tmp0, 2))); // 9 elements
            If(Compare(Global.tmp1, >, Null)); // 5 elements
                "Preserve spent ammo when revisiting a loadout; no free reload on weapon swaps."
                Set Global Variable(tmp2, Add(Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0), Max(False, Subtract(Global.tmp1, Max Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0))))); // 14 elements
                "Freeze the persistent capacity action's inputs before the loop or\nother rules reuse these global scratch slots (including after reloads)."
                Set Max Ammo(Event Player, Evaluate Once(Global.tmp0), Evaluate Once(Global.tmp1)); // 7 elements
                Set Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0, Global.tmp2); // 3 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        "Give the engine a frame before priming the refill fallback. Cached player\nstate survives this wait; no global scratch value is retained across it."
        Wait(False, Abort When False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, If-Then-Else(Or(And(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, 2), >, Max Ammo(Event Player, False)), Compare(Max Ammo(Event Player, False), >, Null)), And(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, 3), >, Max Ammo(Event Player, True)), Compare(Max Ammo(Event Player, True), >, Null))), Array(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, False), <, Max Ammo(Event Player, False)), Compare(Ammo(Event Player, True), <, Max Ammo(Event Player, True)), Is Reloading(Event Player), Ammo(Event Player, False), Ammo(Event Player, True)), Null)); // 65 elements
        Wait Until(Or(Or(Or(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), !=, First Of((Event Player).augmentState1)), Compare(Weapon(Event Player), !=, Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, True))), Not(Has Spawned(Event Player))), Is Dead(Event Player)), 999999999999); // 26 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//249 elements
rule ("AMMO PACK REFILL FALLBACK") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).augmentId == 5; // 4 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
        Hero Of(Event Player) == First Of((Event Player).augmentState1); // 5 elements
        Weapon(Event Player) == Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, True); // 6 elements
        (Event Player).augmentState2 != Null; // 3 elements
        Or(Or(Compare(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, False), <, Max Ammo(Event Player, False)), !=, First Of((Event Player).augmentState2)), Compare(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, True), <, Max Ammo(Event Player, True)), !=, Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, True))), Compare(Is Reloading(Event Player), !=, Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, 2))) == True; // 41 elements
    }
    actions {
        "Some heroes (including Widowmaker) accept Set Ammo but ignore Set Max Ammo.\nWatch only the native-full boundary and reload edges, not every fired round."
        If(And(Is Reloading(Event Player), Not(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, 2)))); // 10 elements
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, augmentState2, 3, Ammo(Event Player, False)); // 4 elements
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, augmentState2, 4, Ammo(Event Player, True)); // 4 elements
        End; // 1 element
        "These temporary inputs are synchronous; the handler has no waits/captures."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, 2, True); // 3 elements
            Set Global Variable(tmp1, Max Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0)); // 4 elements
            If(And(And(Compare(Global.tmp1, >, Null), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, Add(Global.tmp0, 2)), >, Global.tmp1)), Or(And(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0), ==, Global.tmp1), Or(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, Global.tmp0), And(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, 2), Not(Is Reloading(Event Player))))), And(And(And(And(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Widowmaker)), Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, 2)), Not(Is Reloading(Event Player))), Compare(Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0), <, Global.tmp1)), Or(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0), >, Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, Add(Global.tmp0, 3))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, Add(Global.tmp0, 3)), >, Global.tmp1)))))); // 103 elements
                "while firing, and cancelled/partial reloads, must not grant ammo.\nWidow can fire on the frame her reload finishes. Restore the bonus\nwhile preserving those spent rounds (34/35 becomes 69/35)."
                Set Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0, Subtract(Add(Ammo(Event Player, Global.tmp0), Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState1, Add(Global.tmp0, 2))), Global.tmp1)); // 18 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, Array(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, False), <, Max Ammo(Event Player, False)), Compare(Ammo(Event Player, True), <, Max Ammo(Event Player, True)), Is Reloading(Event Player), Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, 3), Value In Array((Event Player).augmentState2, 4))); // 32 elements
    }
}

//167 elements
rule ("LASER BARRICADE (PLACEMENT)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).augmentId == 7; // 4 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Crouch)) == True; // 4 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).augmentState2 == Null; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        "Negative cooldown latches aiming without another player variable. Only this\nrule owns it; clear it on every exit before starting the normal cooldown."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, -1); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Or(Not(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact))), Is Dead(Event Player)), 0.5); // 9 elements
        If(Or(Not(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact))), Is Dead(Event Player))); // 8 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, Null); // 1 element
            Abort; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Play Effect(Event Player, Good Explosion, Color(Blue), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Multiply(Direction From Angles(Add(Horizontal Facing Angle Of(Event Player), Random Real(-5, 5)), Add(Vertical Facing Angle Of(Event Player), Random Real(-5, 5))), 1.5)), 0.1); // 25 elements
        Play Effect(Event Player, Buff Explosion Sound, Color(Blue), Position Of(Event Player), 75); // 4 elements
        Wait Until(Or(Not(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact))), Is Dead(Event Player)), 999999999999); // 9 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, Null); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(checkEnhancementPlacement); // 1 element
        Abort If(Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
        "Laser Barrier health"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentBarrierHealth, 10000); // 2 elements
        "Barrier center point"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState3, Ray Cast Hit Position(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Multiply(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), 30)), Null, All Players(All Teams), False)); // 16 elements
        "Barrier corner 1"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot26, Add((Event Player).augmentState3, World Vector Of(Multiply(-2.5, Left), Event Player, Rotation))); // 11 elements
        "Barrier Corner 2"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, actorTeamSlot27, Add((Event Player).augmentState3, World Vector Of(Multiply(2.5, Left), Event Player, Rotation))); // 11 elements
        "Horizontal push direction captured at placement; reference points are center +/- this vector."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState0, Direction From Angles(Horizontal Facing Angle Of(Event Player), 0)); // 5 elements
        "Along-barrier direction, reused by collision checks."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState1, Direction Towards((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26, (Event Player).actorTeamSlot27)); // 7 elements
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring Explosion, Color(Blue), (Event Player).augmentState3, 2); // 7 elements
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(Blue), (Event Player).augmentState3, 50); // 7 elements
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("  Barricade placed!")); // 5 elements
        "Laser Barrier cooldown"
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, 30); // 2 elements
        While(Compare((Event Player).augmentState2, >, Null)); // 6 elements
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState2, Add, -1); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//398 elements
rule ("LASER BARRICADE BLOCKS ZOMBIES") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).augmentId == 7; // 4 elements
        "Barrier health is greater than 0"
        (Event Player).augmentBarrierHealth > Null; // 3 elements
        "Horizontal distance check"
        Is True For Any(All Living Players(Team 2), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).augmentState3, Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).augmentState3)), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)))), <, 2.5)) == True; // 24 elements
        "If feet are below highest point of wall"
        Is True For Any(All Living Players(Team 2), Compare(Y Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), <, Add(Y Component Of((Event Player).augmentState3), 2))) == True; // 16 elements
        "If eyes are above lowest point of wall"
        Is True For Any(All Living Players(Team 2), Compare(Y Component Of(Eye Position(Current Array Element)), >, Y Component Of((Event Player).augmentState3))) == True; // 13 elements
        "Distance from wall"
        Is True For Any(All Living Players(Team 2), Compare(Distance Between(Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element))), Add((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26, Multiply((Event Player).augmentState1, Min(5, Absolute Value(Dot Product((Event Player).augmentState1, Subtract(Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element))), (Event Player).actorTeamSlot26))))))), <, 1.5)) == True; // 51 elements
    }
    actions {
        Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, augmentBarrierHealth); // 1 element
        "Filter horizontal distance, feet height, eye height and distance from wall together."
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, candidateTargetList, Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), And(And(And(Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).augmentState3, Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).augmentState3)), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)))), <, 2.5), Compare(Y Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), <, Add(Y Component Of((Event Player).augmentState3), 2))), Compare(Y Component Of(Eye Position(Current Array Element)), >, Y Component Of((Event Player).augmentState3))), Compare(Distance Between(Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element))), Add((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26, Multiply((Event Player).augmentState1, Min(5, Absolute Value(Dot Product((Event Player).augmentState1, Subtract(Vector(X Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element)), Y Component Of((Event Player).actorTeamSlot26), Z Component Of(Position Of(Current Array Element))), (Event Player).actorTeamSlot26))))))), <, 1.5)))); // 95 elements
        "barrierPushReferencePointList: both push reference points, reconstructed from saved geometry and consumed before wait."
        Set Global Variable(tmp1, Array(Add((Event Player).augmentState3, (Event Player).augmentState0), Add((Event Player).augmentState3, Multiply((Event Player).augmentState0, -1)))); // 19 elements
        "barrierCollisionTargetIndex: barricade target index, to damage and push each zombie crossing the barrier."
        For Global Variable(tmp0, 0, Count Of((Event Player).candidateTargetList), True); // 5 elements
            "barrierCollisionTarget: current zombie, consumed by this iteration's damage/push actions before wait."
            Set Global Variable(tmp2, Value In Array((Event Player).candidateTargetList, Global.tmp0)); // 6 elements
            Skip If(Has Status(Global.tmp2, Rooted), 3); // 5 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, augmentBarrierHealth, Subtract, Multiply(Max Health(Global.tmp2), 2.5)); // 6 elements
            Set Status(Global.tmp2, Event Player, Rooted, 0.5); // 3 elements
            Damage(Global.tmp2, Event Player, 10); // 3 elements
            //__label_if_83__:
            If(Not(Magnitude Of(Throttle Of(Global.tmp2)))); // 5 elements
                "If bot is not throttling"
                Apply Impulse(Global.tmp2, Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards((Event Player).augmentState3, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.tmp1, Distance Between(Global.tmp2, Current Array Element))))), Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Y Component Of(Position Of(Global.tmp2)), >, Y Component Of((Event Player).augmentState3)), -1, 1), Add(90, Vertical Angle From Direction(Ray Cast Hit Normal(Position Of(Global.tmp2), Add(Position Of(Global.tmp2), Down), Null, All Players(All Teams), False))))), 1, To World, Incorporate Contrary Motion); // 50 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                "If bot is throttling"
                Apply Impulse(Global.tmp2, Direction From Angles(Horizontal Angle From Direction(Direction Towards((Event Player).augmentState3, Last Of(Sorted Array(Global.tmp1, Angle Between Vectors(World Vector Of(Throttle Of(Global.tmp2), Global.tmp2, Rotation), Direction Towards((Event Player).augmentState3, Current Array Element)))))), Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Y Component Of(Position Of(Global.tmp2)), >, Y Component Of((Event Player).augmentState3)), -1, 1), Add(90, Vertical Angle From Direction(Ray Cast Hit Normal(Position Of(Global.tmp2), Add(Position Of(Global.tmp2), Down), Null, All Players(All Teams), False))))), 10, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion); // 59 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition); // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
        If(Compare((Event Player).augmentBarrierHealth, >, Null)); // 6 elements
            Wait(3, Restart When True); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Wait(6, Restart When True); // 2 elements
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).augmentBarrierHealth, >, Null), True); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, augmentBarrierHealth, 1); // 2 elements
            //__label_if_84__:
        End; // 1 element
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, augmentBarrierHealth, 10000, 500, None); // 3 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//48 elements
rule ("SCAVENGER BOT LOOT") {
    event {
        Player Earned Elimination;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.isGameplayActive == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).augmentId == 9; // 4 elements
        Team Of(Victim) == Team 2; // 3 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Victim) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Random Integer(True, 20), ==, 1)); // 8 elements
            "One item roll per credited kill or damage participation. No waits or\ncalls while this scratch value is live; reserves never activate here."
            Set Global Variable(tmp0, Random Integer(True, 13)); // 4 elements
            If(Compare(Count Of((Event Player).augmentState0), <, 2)); // 8 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, augmentState0, Append To Array, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
                Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Scavenger: powerup added to backpack")); // 5 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(supplyPowerUpIdList, Append To Array, Global.tmp0); // 2 elements
                Modify Global Variable(supplyPowerUpPositionList, Append To Array, Add(Nearest Walkable Position(Position Of(Victim)), Global.offsetUpQuarterConst)); // 6 elements
    }
}

//15 elements
rule ("LEECH LIFESTEAL") {
    event {
        Player Dealt Damage;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    actions {
        "Workshop workaround: either eligibility check in rule Conditions reproduced\ndisappearing newer heroes during voting. Keep both checks in Actions instead;\nthe engine cause is unknown. isGameplayActive only holds boolean/default zero."
        If(And(Global.isGameplayActive, Compare((Event Player).augmentId, ==, 10))); // 10 elements
            "Include damage from the hero, primary enhancement and scripted powerups."
            Heal(Event Player, Event Player, Multiply(Event Damage, 0.15)); // 4 elements
    }
}
